============================================================
FILE: lang/translate.php
============================================================
<?php
// lang/translate.php
if (session_status() == PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Set default language if not set
if (!isset($_SESSION['lang'])) {
    $_SESSION['lang'] = 'ar'; // Default to Arabic as requested
}

// Handle language change request
if (isset($_GET['lang'])) {
    $lang = $_GET['lang'];
    if ($lang == 'en' || $lang == 'ar') {
        $_SESSION['lang'] = $lang;
        // Redirect back to the same page without the lang parameter to avoid issues on refresh/form submission
        $current_page = basename($_SERVER['PHP_SELF']);
        $query_params = $_GET;
        unset($query_params['lang']);
        $redirect_url = $current_page . (!empty($query_params) ? '?' . http_build_query($query_params) : '');
        header("Location: " . $redirect_url);
        exit();
    }
}

// Load the selected language file
$lang_file = __DIR__ . '/' . $_SESSION['lang'] . '.php';
if (file_exists($lang_file)) {
    $translations = include($lang_file);
} else {
    // Fallback to Arabic if language file not found
    $translations = include(__DIR__ . '/ar.php');
}

// Translation function
function t($key)
{
    global $translations;
    return isset($translations[$key]) ? $translations[$key] : $key; // Return key if translation not found
}

// Function to get current language direction
function get_direction()
{
    return ($_SESSION['lang'] == 'ar') ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
}

// Function to get current language code
function get_lang_code()
{
    return $_SESSION['lang'];
}


============================================================
FILE: add_coastal_property.php
============================================================
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . 
'/lang/translate.php'; // Translation handler
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system
require_once 'session_check.php'; // Include session timeout check
require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php'; // Database connection

if (session_status() == PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Check permissions - only manager and admin can add properties

// --- Fetch Lookups (Similar to add_residential) ---
$property_types = [];
$unit_designs = [];
$finishing_types = [];
$employees = [];

// Fetch Property Types (Filter for Coastal Type = 2 - Assuming 2 is Coastal)
$sql_types = "SELECT PropertyTypeID, TypeName FROM PropertyTypes WHERE PropertyTypeID = 2";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_types)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $property_types[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching coastal property types: " . $conn->error);
}

// Fetch Unit Designs
$sql_designs = "SELECT DesignID, DesignName FROM propertyunitdesigns ORDER BY DesignID";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_designs)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $unit_designs[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching unit designs: " . $conn->error);
}

// Fetch Finishing Types
$sql_finishing = "SELECT FinishingID, FinishingName FROM propertyfinishingtypes ORDER BY FinishingID";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_finishing)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $finishing_types[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching finishing types: " . $conn->error);
}

// Fetch Employees
$sql_employees = "SELECT EmployeeID, FullName FROM employees ORDER BY FullName";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_employees)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $employees[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching employees: " . $conn->error);
}

$errors = [];
$success_message = '';

if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST") {
    // --- Data Validation and Sanitization (Similar to Residential) ---
    $property_type_id = 2; // Hardcoded for Coastal page
    $project_name = trim($_POST['project_name'] ?? '');
    $title = trim($_POST['title'] ?? ''); // Optional Title
    $description = trim($_POST['description'] ?? '');
    $governorate = trim($_POST['governorate'] ?? '');
    $district = trim($_POST['district'] ?? '');
    $location = trim($_POST['location'] ?? ''); // Detailed Address
    $company_name = trim($_POST['company_name'] ?? '');
    $compound_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'compound_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE); // Coastal has compound area
    $floors_count = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'floors_count', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $unit_design_id = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'unit_design_id', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $min_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'min_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $max_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'max_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $avg_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'avg_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    // Removed bedrooms field as requested
    $bathrooms = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'bathrooms', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $finishing_type_id = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'finishing_type_id', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $min_price = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'min_price', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $max_price = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'max_price', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $price = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'price', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $down_payment_percentage = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'down_payment_percentage', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $payment_years = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'payment_years', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $delivery_date_str = trim($_POST['delivery_date'] ?? '');
    $status = trim($_POST['status'] ?? 'available');
    // Auto-assign to current employee instead of selection
    $employee_id = $_SESSION['employee_id'] ?? null;
    // $is_featured = isset($_POST['is_featured']) ? 1 : 0; // Removed is_featured
    $sales_manager_name = trim($_POST['sales_manager_name'] ?? '');
    $sales_manager_phone = trim($_POST['sales_manager_phone'] ?? '');
    $salesperson_name = trim($_POST['salesperson_name'] ?? '');
    $salesperson_phone = trim($_POST['salesperson_phone'] ?? '');

    // --- Basic Validation (Similar to Residential) ---
    if (empty($project_name) && empty($title)) {
        $errors[] = t('error_project_or_title_required');
    }
    if (empty($governorate)) {
        $errors[] = t('error_governorate_required');
    }
    if (empty($district)) {
        $errors[] = t('error_district_required');
    }
    if ($unit_design_id === null) {
        $errors[] = t('error_unit_design_required');
    }
    if ($finishing_type_id === null) {
        $errors[] = t('error_finishing_type_required');
    }
    if ($area === null && $min_area === null && $max_area === null && $avg_area === null) {
        $errors[] = t('error_area_required');
    }
    if ($price === null && $min_price === null && $max_price === null) {
        $errors[] = t("error_price_required");
    }

    // Basic validation for phone numbers (optional, can be enhanced)
    if (!empty($sales_manager_phone) && !preg_match("/^[0-9\+\-\s\(\)]+$/", $sales_manager_phone)) {
        $errors[] = t("invalid_phone_format");
    }
    if (!empty($salesperson_phone) && !preg_match("/^[0-9\+\-\s\(\)]+$/", $salesperson_phone)) {
        $errors[] = t("invalid_phone_format");
    }

    // Validate delivery date format if provided
    $delivery_date = null;
    if (!empty($delivery_date_str)) {
        $date_obj = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $delivery_date_str);
        if ($date_obj && $date_obj->format('Y-m-d') === $delivery_date_str) {
            $delivery_date = $delivery_date_str;
        } else {
            $errors[] = t('error_invalid_delivery_date');
        }
    }

    // --- Database Insertion (Updated SQL to remove bedrooms) ---
    if (empty($errors)) {
        $sql = "INSERT INTO Properties (
                    PropertyTypeID, ProjectName, Title, Description, Location, 
                    governorate, district, company_name, compound_area, FloorsCount, 
                    unit_design_id, min_area, max_area, avg_area, Area, 
                    Bathrooms, finishing_type_id, min_price, max_price, 
                    Price, down_payment_percentage, payment_years, delivery_date, Status, 
                    EmployeeID, 
                    sales_manager_name, sales_manager_phone, salesperson_name, salesperson_phone, 
                    DateAdded
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NOW())";

        if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
            // Adjusted bind_param types - removed bedrooms parameter, added sales fields
            $stmt->bind_param(
                "isssssssdiiddddiddddisssissss",
                $property_type_id,
                $project_name,
                $title,
                $description,
                $location,
                $governorate,
                $district,
                $company_name,
                $compound_area,
                $floors_count,
                $unit_design_id,
                $min_area,
                $max_area,
                $avg_area,
                $area,
                $bathrooms,
                $finishing_type_id,
                $min_price,
                $max_price,
                $price,
                $down_payment_percentage,
                $payment_years,
                $delivery_date,
                $status,
                $employee_id,
                $sales_manager_name,
                $sales_manager_phone,
                $salesperson_name,
                $salesperson_phone
            );

            if ($stmt->execute()) {
                $_SESSION["success_message"] = t('success_property_added');
                header('Location: coastal_properties.php');
                exit();           } else {
                $errors[] = t('error_adding_property') . " " . $stmt->error;
                error_log("Error executing property insert: " . $stmt->error);
            }
            $stmt->close();
        } else {
            $errors[] = t('database_error') . " " . $conn->error;
            error_log("Error preparing property insert: " . $conn->error);
        }
    }
    $conn->close();
}

        <!-- Property Type (Hidden/Fixed for this page) -->

        <!-- Form Sections (Identical structure to Residential, including Compound Area) -->
        
        <div class="form-section">
            <h3><?php echo t('basic_info'); ?></h3>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="title"><?php echo t('unit_title_optional'); ?>:</label>
                    <input type="text" id="title" name="title" class="form-input" value="<?php echo htmlspecialchars($_POST['title'] ?? ''); ?>">
                </div>
                 <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="company_name"><?php echo t('company'); ?>:</label>
                    <input type="text" id="company_name" name="company_name" class="form-input" value="<?php echo htmlspecialchars($_POST['company_name'] ?? ''); ?>">
                </div>
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="status"><?php echo t('status'); ?>:</label>
                    <select id="status" name="status" class="form-input">
                        <option value="available" <?php echo (($_POST['status'] ?? 'available') == 'available') ? 'selected' : ''; ?>><?php echo t('available'); ?></option>
                        <option value="sold" <?php echo (($_POST['status'] ?? '') == 'sold') ? 'selected' : ''; ?>><?php echo t('sold'); ?></option>
                        <option value="rented" <?php echo (($_POST['status'] ?? '') == 'rented') ? 'selected' : ''; ?>><?php echo t('rented'); ?></option>
                        <option value="unavailable" <?php echo (($_POST['status'] ?? '') == 'unavailable') ? 'selected' : ''; ?>><?php echo t('unavailable'); ?></option>
                    </select>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="form-section">
            <h3><?php echo t('location_info'); ?></h3>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/lang/translate.php'; // Include translation handler
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system
require_once 'session_check.php'; // Include session timeout check

if (session_status() == PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Check permissions - only manager can manage employees
check_permission("manage_employees");
require_once __DIR__ . 
'/db.php'; // Database connection

$full_name = $username = $password = $role = "";
$full_name_err = $username_err = $password_err = $role_err = $general_err = "";

if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST") {
    // Validate Full Name
    if (empty(trim($_POST["fullName"]))) {
        $full_name_err = "Please enter the employee's full name.";
    } else {
        $full_name = trim($_POST["fullName"]);
    }

    // Validate Username
    if (empty(trim($_POST["username"]))) {
        $username_err = "Please enter a username.";
    } elseif (!preg_match('/^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$/', trim($_POST["username"]))) {
        $username_err = "Username can only contain letters, numbers, and underscores.";
    } else {
        // Check if username already exists
        $sql_check_username = "SELECT EmployeeID FROM Employees WHERE Username = ?";
        if ($stmt_check = $conn->prepare($sql_check_username)) {
            $stmt_check->bind_param("s", $param_username_check);
            $param_username_check = trim($_POST["username"]);
            if ($stmt_check->execute()) {
                $stmt_check->store_result();
                if ($stmt_check->num_rows > 0) {
                    $username_err = "This username is already taken.";
                }
                $username = trim($_POST["username"]);
            } else {
                $general_err = "Error checking username. Please try again.";
                error_log("Error executing username check: " . $stmt_check->error);
            }
            $stmt_check->close();
        } else {
            $general_err = "Database error (username check). Please try again.";
            error_log("Error preparing username check: " . $conn->error);
        }
    }

    // Validate Password
    if (empty($_POST["password"])) {
        $password_err = "Please enter a password.";
    } elseif (strlen($_POST["password"]) < 6) {
        $password_err = "Password must have at least 6 characters.";
    } else {
        $password = $_POST["password"];
    }

    // Validate Role
    if (empty($_POST["role"])) {
        $role_err = "Please select a role.";
    } elseif (!in_array($_POST["role"], ["admin", "user", "manager"])) {
        $role_err = "Invalid role selected.";
    } else {
        $role = $_POST["role"];
    }

    // Check input errors before inserting in database
    if (empty($full_name_err) && empty($username_err) && empty($password_err) && empty($role_err) && empty($general_err)) {
        // Hash the password
        $hashed_password = password_hash($password, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);

        $sql = "INSERT INTO Employees (FullName, Username, Password, Role) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)";

        if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
            $stmt->bind_param("ssss", $param_full_name, $param_username, $param_hashed_password, $param_role);
            
            $param_full_name = $full_name;
            $param_username = $username;
            $param_hashed_password = $hashed_password;
            $param_role = $role;
            
            if ($stmt->execute()) {
                $_SESSION["success_message"] = "Employee added successfully!";
                header("Location: employees_list.php"); // Redirect to employees list or dashboard
                exit();
            } else {
                $general_err = "Something went wrong. Please try again later.";
                error_log("Error executing employee insert: " . $stmt->error);
            }
            $stmt->close();
        }
    }
}
?>

                <?php if (!empty($general_err)): ?>

                <?php endif; ?>

                <div>
                    <label for="fullName"><?php echo t('full_name'); ?>:</label>
                    <input type="text" id="fullName" name="fullName" class="form-input <?php echo (!empty($full_name_err)) ? 'is-invalid' : ''; ?>" value="<?php echo htmlspecialchars($full_name); ?>" required>
                    <?php if (!empty($full_name_err)): ?><span class="error-message"><?php echo htmlspecialchars($full_name_err); ?></span><?php endif; ?>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label for="username"><?php echo t('username'); ?>:</label>
                    <input type="text" id="username" name="username" class="form-input <?php echo (!empty($username_err)) ? 'is-invalid' : ''; ?>" value="<?php echo htmlspecialchars($username); ?>" required>
                    <?php if (!empty($username_err)): ?><span class="error-message"><?php echo htmlspecialchars($username_err); ?></span><?php endif; ?>
                </div>

                <div>

                    <?php if (!empty($password_err)): ?><span class="error-message"><?php echo htmlspecialchars($password_err); ?></span><?php endif; ?>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <label for="role"><?php echo t('role'); ?>:</label>
                    <select id="role" name="role" class="form-input <?php echo (!empty($role_err)) ? 'is-invalid' : ''; ?>" required>
                        <option value=""><?php echo t('select_role'); ?></option>
                        <option value="user" <?php echo ($role == "user") ? "selected" : ""; ?>><?php echo t('user_role'); ?></option>
                        <option value="admin" <?php echo ($role == "admin") ? "selected" : ""; ?>><?php echo t('admin_role'); ?></option>
                        <option value="manager" <?php echo ($role == "manager") ? "selected" : ""; ?>><?php echo t('manager_role'); ?></option>
                    </select>
                    <?php if (!empty($role_err)): ?><span class="error-message"><?php echo htmlspecialchars($role_err); ?></span><?php endif; ?>
                </div>

                <div class="form-actions">
                    <input type="submit" value="<?php echo t('add_new_employee'); ?>" class="btn btn-success">
                    <a href="employees_list.php" class="btn btn-secondary"><?php echo t('cancel'); ?></a>
                </div>
            </form>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . 
'/lang/translate.php'; // Translation handler
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system
require_once 'session_check.php'; // Include session timeout check
require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php'; // Database connection

if (session_status() == PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Check permissions - only manager and admin can add properties

// --- Fetch Lookups ---
$property_types = [];
$finishing_types = [];
$employees = [];

// Fetch Property Types (Filter for Commercial, Admin, Hotel, Medical - IDs 3, 4, 5, 6)
$sql_types = "SELECT PropertyTypeID, TypeName FROM PropertyTypes WHERE PropertyTypeID IN (3, 4, 5, 6) ORDER BY PropertyTypeID";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_types)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $property_types[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching other property types: " . $conn->error);
}

// Fetch Finishing Types
$sql_finishing = "SELECT FinishingID, FinishingName FROM propertyfinishingtypes ORDER BY FinishingID";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_finishing)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $finishing_types[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching finishing types: " . $conn->error);
}

// Fetch Employees
$sql_employees = "SELECT EmployeeID, FullName FROM employees ORDER BY FullName";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_employees)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $employees[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching employees: " . $conn->error);
}

$errors = [];
$success_message = '';

if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST") {
    // --- Data Validation and Sanitization ---
    $property_type_id = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'property_type_id', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
    $project_name = trim($_POST['project_name'] ?? '');
    $title = trim($_POST['title'] ?? '');
    $description = trim($_POST['description'] ?? '');
    $governorate = trim($_POST['governorate'] ?? '');
    $district = trim($_POST['district'] ?? '');
    $location = trim($_POST['location'] ?? '');
    $company_name = trim($_POST['company_name'] ?? '');
    $floors_count = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'floors_count', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
    if ($floors_count === false) {
        $floors_count = null;
    }
    $min_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'min_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $max_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'max_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $avg_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'avg_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $finishing_type_id_raw = $_POST['finishing_type_id'] ?? '';
    $finishing_type_id = is_numeric($finishing_type_id_raw) && $finishing_type_id_raw !== '' ? (int)$finishing_type_id_raw : null;
    $min_price = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'min_price', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $max_price = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'max_price', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $price = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'price', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $down_payment_percentage = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'down_payment_percentage', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $payment_years = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'payment_years', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
    $delivery_date_str = trim($_POST['delivery_date'] ?? '');
    $status = trim($_POST['status'] ?? 'available');
    $employee_id = $_SESSION['id'] ?? null; // Use 'id' from your session if that's the key
    $sales_manager_name = trim($_POST['sales_manager_name'] ?? '');
    $sales_manager_phone = trim($_POST['sales_manager_phone'] ?? '');
    $salesperson_name = trim($_POST['salesperson_name'] ?? '');
    $salesperson_phone = trim($_POST['salesperson_phone'] ?? '');

    // --- Basic Validation ---
    // (Your validation code here, unchanged)
    if (empty($project_name) && empty($title)) {
        $errors[] = t('error_project_or_title_required');
    }
    // ... etc.

    // Validate delivery date format if provided
    $delivery_date = null;
    if (!empty($delivery_date_str)) {
        $date_obj = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $delivery_date_str);
        if ($date_obj && $date_obj->format('Y-m-d') === $delivery_date_str) {
            $delivery_date = $delivery_date_str;
        } else {
            $errors[] = t('error_invalid_delivery_date');
        }
    }

// --- Database Insertion
$sql = "INSERT INTO Properties (
    PropertyTypeID, Title, Description, Location,
    governorate, district, company_name, FloorsCount,
    min_area, max_area, avg_area, Area,
    finishing_type_id, min_price, max_price,
    Price, down_payment_percentage, payment_years, delivery_date, Status,
    EmployeeID,
    sales_manager_name, sales_manager_phone, salesperson_name, salesperson_phone,
    ProjectName
) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";

$date_added = date("Y-m-d H:i:s");

$params = array(
    $property_type_id,
    $title,
    $description,
    $location,
    $governorate,
    $district,
    $company_name,
    $floors_count,
    $min_area,
    $max_area,
    $avg_area,
    $area,
    $finishing_type_id,
    $min_price,
    $max_price,
    $price,
    $down_payment_percentage,
    $payment_years,
    $delivery_date,
    $status,
    $employee_id,
    $sales_manager_name,
    $sales_manager_phone,
    $salesperson_name,
    $salesperson_phone,
    $project_name,
    
);


$types = "issssssiddiddiddississssss"; // ✔️ طوله = 26


error_log("DEBUG: \$types string: " . $types);
error_log("DEBUG: Length of \$types: " . strlen($types));
error_log("DEBUG: \$params array count: " . count($params));
error_log("DEBUG: \$params array content: " . var_export($params, true));

if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
    if (count($params) !== strlen($types)) {
        error_log("Mismatch: Expected " . strlen($types) . " params, got " . count($params));
        $errors[] = t('database_error');
    } else {
        $stmt->bind_param(
            $types,
            ...$params
        );

        if ($stmt->execute()) {
            $_SESSION["success_message"] = t('success_property_added');
            header("Location: commercial_properties.php");
            exit();
        } else {
            $errors[] = t('error_adding_property') . " " . $stmt->error;
            error_log("Error executing property insert: " . $stmt->error);
        }
    }
    $stmt->close();
} else {
    $errors[] = t('database_error') . " " . $conn->error;
    error_log("Error preparing property insert: " . $conn->error);
}


    $conn->close();
}

============================================================
FILE: add_residential_property.php

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . 
'/lang/translate.php'; // Translation handler
require_once 'session_check.php'; // Include session timeout check
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system
require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php'; // Database connection

if (session_status() == PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Check permissions - only manager and admin can add properties
check_permission('manage_properties');

$property_types = [];
$unit_designs = [];
$finishing_types = [];
$employees = [];

// Fetch Property Types (Filter for Residential Type = 1)
$sql_types = "SELECT PropertyTypeID, TypeName FROM PropertyTypes WHERE PropertyTypeID = 1"; // Assuming 1 is Residential
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_types)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $property_types[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching residential property types: " . $conn->error);
}

// Fetch Unit Designs
$sql_designs = "SELECT DesignID, DesignName FROM propertyunitdesigns ORDER BY DesignID";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_designs)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $unit_designs[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching unit designs: " . $conn->error);
}

// Fetch Finishing Types
$sql_finishing = "SELECT FinishingID, FinishingName FROM propertyfinishingtypes ORDER BY FinishingID";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_finishing)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $finishing_types[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching finishing types: " . $conn->error);
}

// Fetch Employees (for assignment)
$sql_employees = "SELECT EmployeeID, FullName FROM employees ORDER BY FullName";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_employees)) {
    while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
        $employees[] = $row;
    }
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching employees: " . $conn->error);
}


$errors = [];
$success_message = '';

if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST") {
    // --- Data Validation and Sanitization ---
    $property_type_id = 1; // Hardcoded for Residential page
    $project_name = trim($_POST['project_name'] ?? '');
    $title = trim($_POST['title'] ?? ''); // Optional Title
    $description = trim($_POST['description'] ?? '');
    $governorate = trim($_POST['governorate'] ?? '');
    $district = trim($_POST['district'] ?? '');
    $location = trim($_POST['location'] ?? ''); // Detailed Address
    $company_name = trim($_POST['company_name'] ?? '');
    $compound_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'compound_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $floors_count = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'floors_count', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $unit_design_id = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'unit_design_id', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $min_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'min_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $max_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'max_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $avg_area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'avg_area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $area = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'area', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE); // Specific area if no range
    // Removed bedrooms field as requested
    $bathrooms = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'bathrooms', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $finishing_type_id = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'finishing_type_id', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $min_price = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'min_price', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $max_price = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'max_price', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $price = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'price', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE); // Specific price if no range
    $down_payment_percentage = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'down_payment_percentage', FILTER_VALIDATE_FLOAT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $payment_years = filter_input(INPUT_POST, 'payment_years', FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, FILTER_NULL_ON_FAILURE);
    $delivery_date_str = trim($_POST['delivery_date'] ?? '');
    $status = trim($_POST['status'] ?? 'available');
    // Auto-assign to current employee instead of selection
    $employee_id = $_SESSION['employee_id'] ?? null;
    // $is_featured = isset($_POST['is_featured']) ? 1 : 0; // Removed is_featured
    $sales_manager_name = trim($_POST['sales_manager_name'] ?? '');
    $sales_manager_phone = trim($_POST['sales_manager_phone'] ?? '');
    $salesperson_name = trim($_POST['salesperson_name'] ?? '');
    $salesperson_phone = trim($_POST['salesperson_phone'] ?? '');

    // --- Basic Validation ---
    if (empty($project_name) && empty($title)) {
        $errors[] = t('error_project_or_title_required');
    }
    if (empty($governorate)) {
        $errors[] = t('error_governorate_required');
    }
    if (empty($district)) {
        $errors[] = t('error_district_required');
    }
    if ($unit_design_id === null) {
        $errors[] = t('error_unit_design_required');
    }
    if ($finishing_type_id === null) {
        $errors[] = t('error_finishing_type_required');
    }
    if ($area === null && $min_area === null && $max_area === null && $avg_area === null) {
        $errors[] = t('error_area_required');
    }
    if ($price === null && $min_price === null && $max_price === null) {
        $errors[] = t("error_price_required");
    }

    // Basic validation for phone numbers (optional, can be enhanced)
    if (!empty($sales_manager_phone) && !preg_match("/^[0-9\+\-\s\(\)]+$/", $sales_manager_phone)) {
        $errors[] = t("invalid_phone_format");
    }
    if (!empty($salesperson_phone) && !preg_match("/^[0-9\+\-\s\(\)]+$/", $salesperson_phone)) {
        $errors[] = t("invalid_phone_format");
    }

    // Validate delivery date format if provided
    $delivery_date = null;
    if (!empty($delivery_date_str)) {
        $date_obj = DateTime::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $delivery_date_str);
        if ($date_obj && $date_obj->format('Y-m-d') === $delivery_date_str) {
            $delivery_date = $delivery_date_str;
        } else {
            $errors[] = t('error_invalid_delivery_date');
        }
    }

    // --- Database Insertion ---
    if (empty($errors)) {
        // Use actual column names from broman_db.sql
        $sql = "INSERT INTO Properties (
                    PropertyTypeID, ProjectName, Title, Description, Location, 
                    governorate, district, company_name, compound_area, FloorsCount, 
                    unit_design_id, min_area, max_area, avg_area, Area, 
                    Bathrooms, finishing_type_id, min_price, max_price, 
                    Price, down_payment_percentage, payment_years, delivery_date, Status, 
                    EmployeeID, 
                    sales_manager_name, sales_manager_phone, salesperson_name, salesperson_phone, 
                    DateAdded
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NOW())";

        if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
            // EmployeeID (i), sales_manager_name (s), sales_manager_phone (s), salesperson_name (s), salesperson_phone (s)
            $stmt->bind_param(
                "isssssssdiiddddiddddisssissss",
                $property_type_id,
                $project_name,
                $title,
                $description,
                $location,
                $governorate,
                $district,
                $company_name,
                $compound_area,
                $floors_count,
                $unit_design_id,
                $min_area,
                $max_area,
                $avg_area,
                $area,
                $bathrooms,
                $finishing_type_id,
                $min_price,
                $max_price,
                $price,
                $down_payment_percentage,
                $payment_years,
                $delivery_date,
                $status,
                $employee_id,
                $sales_manager_name,
                $sales_manager_phone,
                $salesperson_name,
                $salesperson_phone
            );

            if ($stmt->execute()) {
                $_SESSION["success_message"] = t('success_property_added');
                header("Location: residential_properties.php");
                exit();
            } else {
                $errors[] = t('error_adding_property') . " " . $stmt->error;
                error_log("Error executing property insert: " . $stmt->error);
            }
            $stmt->close();
        } else {
            $errors[] = t('database_error') . " " . $conn->error;
            error_log("Error preparing property insert: " . $conn->error);
        }
    }
    $conn->close();
}

                    </select>

============================================================
FILE: add_session_js.sh
============================================================
#!/bin/bash

# Script to add session timeout JavaScript to all protected PHP pages
# This script will add the JavaScript include before the closing </head> tag

PROTECTED_PAGES=(
    "add_client.php"
    "add_coastal_property.php"
    "add_employee.php"
    "add_other_property.php"
    "add_residential_property.php"
    "coastal_properties.php"
    "commercial_properties.php"
    "edit_client.php"
    "edit_employee.php"
    "residential_properties.php"
    "view_property.php"
)

for page in "${PROTECTED_PAGES[@]}"; do
    if [ -f "$page" ]; then
        echo "Updating $page..."
        
        # Check if session_timeout.js is already included
        if ! grep -q "session_timeout.js" "$page"; then
            # Find the line with </head> and add JavaScript before it
            if grep -q "</head>" "$page"; then
                sed -i 's|</head>|    <script src="session_timeout.js"></script>\n</head>|' "$page"
                echo "Added session timeout JavaScript to $page"
            else
                echo "Warning: Could not find </head> tag in $page"
            fi
        else
            echo "$page already has session timeout JavaScript included"
        fi
    else
        echo "Warning: $page not found"
    fi
done

echo "Script completed."

FILE: coastal_properties.php

<?php
session_start();
require_once __DIR__ . '/lang/translate.php'; // Include translation handler
require_once 'session_check.php'; // Include session timeout check
require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php'; // Database connection
require_once __DIR__ . '/sorting_helpers.php'; // Include sorting helper functions
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system
require_once __DIR__ . '/property_image_helper.php'; // Include image helper functions

// Check permissions - all logged in users can view properties

// Define the Property Type ID for this page (Assuming 2 is Coastal - VERIFY IN DB)
const CURRENT_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID = 2;

// Initialize search and filter variables
$search_term = $governorate_filter = $company_filter = $status_filter = $property_type_filter = "";
$min_price_filter = $max_price_filter = $min_area_filter = $max_area_filter = $down_payment_filter = $payment_period_filter = "";
$unit_design_filter = "";
$view_mode = isset($_GET['view']) && $_GET['view'] === 'card' ? 'card' : 'table';

// Initialize sorting variables
$sort_column = isset($_GET['sort']) ? trim($_GET['sort']) : 'DateAdded';
$sort_direction = isset($_GET['direction']) && $_GET['direction'] === 'desc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';

// Validate sort column to prevent SQL injection
$allowed_sort_columns = [
    'PropertyID' => 'p.PropertyID',
    'DateAdded' => 'p.DateAdded',
    'Price' => 'COALESCE(p.Price, p.min_price)',
    'Area' => 'COALESCE(p.Area, p.avg_area)',
    'ProjectName' => 'p.ProjectName',
    'Title' => 'p.Title',
    'governorate' => 'p.governorate',
    'Status' => 'p.Status'
];

if (!array_key_exists($sort_column, $allowed_sort_columns)) {
    $sort_column = 'DateAdded';
}

// Process search and filter inputs
if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "GET") {
    $search_term = isset($_GET['search']) ? trim($_GET['search']) : '';
    $governorate_filter = isset($_GET['governorate']) ? trim($_GET['governorate']) : '';
    $company_filter = isset($_GET['company']) ? trim($_GET['company']) : '';
    $unit_design_filter = isset($_GET['unit_design']) ? trim($_GET['unit_design']) : '';
    $status_filter = isset($_GET['status']) ? trim($_GET['status']) : '';
    $property_type_filter = isset($_GET['property_type']) ? trim($_GET['property_type']) : '';

    // Advanced filters
    $min_price_filter = isset($_GET['min_price']) && is_numeric($_GET['min_price']) ? trim($_GET['min_price']) : '';
    $max_price_filter = isset($_GET['max_price']) && is_numeric($_GET['max_price']) ? trim($_GET['max_price']) : '';
    $min_area_filter = isset($_GET['min_area']) && is_numeric($_GET['min_area']) ? trim($_GET['min_area']) : '';
    $max_area_filter = isset($_GET['max_area']) && is_numeric($_GET['max_area']) ? trim($_GET['max_area']) : '';
    $down_payment_filter = isset($_GET['down_payment']) ? trim($_GET['down_payment']) : '';
    $payment_period_filter = isset($_GET['payment_period']) ? trim($_GET['payment_period']) : '';
}

// Fetch distinct governorates for the filter dropdown
$governorates_list = [];
$sql_governorates = "SELECT DISTINCT governorate FROM Properties WHERE governorate IS NOT NULL AND governorate != '' ORDER BY governorate";
if ($stmt_gov = $conn->prepare($sql_governorates)) {
    if ($stmt_gov->execute()) {
        $result_governorates = $stmt_gov->get_result();
        while ($row_gov = $result_governorates->fetch_assoc()) {
            $governorates_list[] = $row_gov['governorate'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_gov->close();
}

// Fetch distinct companies for the filter dropdown
$companies_list = [];
$sql_companies = "SELECT DISTINCT company_name FROM Properties WHERE company_name IS NOT NULL AND company_name != '' ORDER BY company_name";
if ($stmt_comp = $conn->prepare($sql_companies)) {
    if ($stmt_comp->execute()) {
        $result_companies = $stmt_comp->get_result();
        while ($row_comp = $result_companies->fetch_assoc()) {
            $companies_list[] = $row_comp['company_name'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_comp->close();
}

// Fetch unit designs for the filter dropdown
$unit_designs = [];
$sql_unit_designs = "SELECT DISTINCT pud.DesignID, pud.DesignName 
                     FROM PropertyUnitDesigns pud 
                     JOIN Properties p ON p.unit_design_id = pud.DesignID 
                     ORDER BY pud.DesignName";
if ($stmt_designs = $conn->prepare($sql_unit_designs)) {
    if ($stmt_designs->execute()) {
        $result_designs = $stmt_designs->get_result();
        while ($row_design = $result_designs->fetch_assoc()) {
            $unit_designs[$row_design['DesignID']] = $row_design['DesignName'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_designs->close();
}

// Fetch property types for the filter dropdown
$property_types = [];
$sql_property_types = "SELECT PropertyTypeID, TypeName FROM PropertyTypes ORDER BY TypeName";
if ($stmt_types = $conn->prepare($sql_property_types)) {
    if ($stmt_types->execute()) {
        $result_types = $stmt_types->get_result();
        while ($row_type = $result_types->fetch_assoc()) {
            $property_types[$row_type['PropertyTypeID']] = $row_type['TypeName'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_types->close();
}

// Build the main SQL query with proper joins for unit design and finishing type
$sql_select = "SELECT p.*, pt.TypeName AS PropertyTypeName, e.FullName AS EmployeeName,
               pud.DesignName AS UnitDesignName, pft.FinishingName AS FinishingTypeName
               FROM Properties p
               LEFT JOIN PropertyTypes pt ON p.PropertyTypeID = pt.PropertyTypeID
               LEFT JOIN Employees e ON p.EmployeeID = e.EmployeeID
               LEFT JOIN PropertyUnitDesigns pud ON p.unit_design_id = pud.DesignID
               LEFT JOIN PropertyFinishingTypes pft ON p.finishing_type_id = pft.FinishingID";
$sql_where = [];
$params = [];
$types = "";

// Add search term filter
if (!empty($search_term)) {
    $sql_where[] = "(p.Title LIKE ? OR p.ProjectName LIKE ? OR p.governorate LIKE ? OR p.district LIKE ? OR p.Location LIKE ? OR p.company_name LIKE ? OR pud.DesignName LIKE ?)";
    $search_param = "%" . $search_term . "%";
    array_push($params, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param);
    $types .= "sssssss";
}

// Add property type filter
if (!empty($property_type_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.PropertyTypeID = ?";
    $params[] = $property_type_filter;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Add governorate filter
if (!empty($governorate_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.governorate = ?";
    $params[] = $governorate_filter;
    $types .= "s";
}

// Add company filter
if (!empty($company_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.company_name = ?";
    $params[] = $company_filter;
    $types .= "s";
}

// Add unit design filter
if (!empty($unit_design_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.unit_design_id = ?";
    $params[] = $unit_design_filter;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Add status filter
if (!empty($status_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.Status = ?";
    $params[] = $status_filter;
    $types .= "s";
}

// Add advanced filters
if (!empty($min_price_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Price, p.min_price) >= ?";
    $params[] = $min_price_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($max_price_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Price, p.max_price) <= ?";
    $params[] = $max_price_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($min_area_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Area, p.min_area) >= ?";
    $params[] = $min_area_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($max_area_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Area, p.max_area) <= ?";
    $params[] = $max_area_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($down_payment_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.down_payment_percentage = ?";
    $params[] = $down_payment_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($payment_period_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.payment_years = ?";
    $params[] = $payment_period_filter;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Add property type filter for coastal properties
$sql_where[] = "p.PropertyTypeID = ?";
$params[] = CURRENT_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID;
$types .= "i";

// Combine SQL parts
$sql = $sql_select;
if (!empty($sql_where)) {
    $sql .= " WHERE " . implode(" AND ", $sql_where);
}

// Add sorting
$sql .= " ORDER BY " . $allowed_sort_columns[$sort_column] . " " . strtoupper($sort_direction);

// Prepare and execute the query
$properties = [];
if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
    if (!empty($params)) {
        $stmt->bind_param($types, ...$params);
    }

    if ($stmt->execute()) {
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
            $properties[] = $row;
        }
    } else {
        error_log("Error executing property query: " . $stmt->error . " | SQL: " . $sql . " | Params: " . print_r($params, true));
        $_SESSION['error_message'] = t('database_error');
    }
    $stmt->close();
} else {
    error_log("Error preparing property query: " . $conn->error . " | SQL: " . $sql);
    $_SESSION['error_message'] = t('db_error_prepare');
}

// Function to format price for display
function formatDisplayPrice($row)
{
    if (!empty($row['Price']) && is_numeric($row['Price'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['Price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_price']) && is_numeric($row['min_price']) && !empty($row['max_price']) && is_numeric($row['max_price'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['min_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' - ' . number_format(floatval($row['max_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_price']) && is_numeric($row['min_price'])) {
        return t('starts_from') . ' ' . number_format(floatval($row['min_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    } elseif (!empty($row['max_price']) && is_numeric($row['max_price'])) {
        return t('up_to') . ' ' . number_format(floatval($row['max_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    }
    return t('price_not_specified');
}

// Function to format area for display
function formatDisplayArea($row)
{
    if (!empty($row['Area']) && is_numeric($row['Area'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['Area']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('sqm');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_area']) && is_numeric($row['min_area']) && !empty($row['max_area']) && is_numeric($row['max_area'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['min_area']), 0, '.', ',') . ' - ' . number_format(floatval($row['max_area']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('sqm');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_area']) && is_numeric($row['min_area'])) {
        return t("starts_from") . " " . number_format(floatval($row["min_area"]), 0, ".", ",") . " " . t("sqm");
    } elseif (!empty($row['max_area']) && is_numeric($row['max_area'])) {
        return t("up_to") . " " . number_format(floatval($row["max_area"]), 0, ".", ",") . " " . t("sqm");
    }
    return t('area_not_specified');
}

// Function to format payment terms
function formatPaymentTerms($row)
{
    $terms = [];
    
    if (!empty($row['down_payment_percentage']) && is_numeric($row['down_payment_percentage'])) {
        $terms[] = number_format($row['down_payment_percentage'], 0) . '% ' . t('down_payment_percentage');
    }
    
    if (!empty($row['payment_years']) && is_numeric($row['payment_years'])) {
        $terms[] = $row['payment_years'] . ' ' . t('years');
    }
    
    return !empty($terms) ? implode(' + ', $terms) : t('undefined');
}

// Function to get status badge HTML
function getStatusBadge($status) {
    $status_classes = [
        'available' => 'status-available',
        'sold' => 'status-sold', 
        'rented' => 'status-rented',
        'unavailable' => 'status-unavailable'
    ];
    
    $status_icons = [
        'available' => 'fas fa-check-circle',
        'sold' => 'fas fa-handshake',
        'rented' => 'fas fa-key',
        'unavailable' => 'fas fa-times-circle'
    ];
    
    $class = $status_classes[$status] ?? 'status-unavailable';
    $icon = $status_icons[$status] ?? 'fas fa-question-circle';
    $text = t('status_' . $status);
    
    return "<span class=\"status-badge {$class}\"><i class=\"{$icon}\"></i> {$text}</span>";
}

// Function to get property type badge HTML
function getPropertyTypeBadge($typeId, $typeName) {
    $type_icons = [
        1 => 'fas fa-home',      // سكني
        2 => 'fas fa-umbrella-beach', // ساحلي
        3 => 'fas fa-building',  // إداري
        4 => 'fas fa-store',     // تجاري
        5 => 'fas fa-hotel',     // فندقي
        6 => 'fas fa-hospital'   // طبي
    ];
    
    $icon = $type_icons[$typeId] ?? 'fas fa-landmark';
    
    return "<span class=\"property-type-badge\"><i class=\"{$icon}\"></i> {$typeName}</span>";
}

<script>
    function toggleAdvancedFilters() {
        const advancedFilters = document.getElementById('advancedFilters');
        const toggleButton = document.querySelector('.advanced-filters-toggle');
        
        if (advancedFilters.classList.contains('show')) {
            advancedFilters.classList.remove('show');
            toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sliders-h"></i> <?php echo t('advanced_filters'); ?>';
        } else {
            advancedFilters.classList.add('show');
            toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-times"></i> <?php echo t('hide_advanced_filters'); ?>';
        }
    }
    
    // Show advanced filters if any of them are set
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        const hasAdvancedFilters = <?php echo
            !empty($min_price_filter) ||
            !empty($max_price_filter) ||
            !empty($min_area_filter) ||
            !empty($max_area_filter) ||
            !empty($down_payment_filter) ||
            !empty($payment_period_filter) ? 'true' : 'false';
        ?>;
        
        if (hasAdvancedFilters) {
            toggleAdvancedFilters();
        }
    });
</script>

<script src="property_row_click.js"></script>

</body>
</html>

// Close the database connection
$conn->close();

============================================================
FILE: commercial_properties.php

<?php
session_start();
require_once __DIR__ . '/lang/translate.php'; // Include translation handler
require_once 'session_check.php'; // Include session timeout check
require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php'; // Database connection
require_once __DIR__ . '/sorting_helpers.php'; // Include sorting helper functions
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system
require_once __DIR__ . '/property_image_helper.php'; // Include image helper functions

// Check permissions - all logged in users can view properties

// Define the Property Type IDs for this page (Commercial/Administrative/Medical/Hotel - VERIFY IN DB)
const CURRENT_PROPERTY_TYPE_IDS = [3, 4, 5, 6]; // Adjust based on your database

// Initialize search and filter variables
$search_term = $governorate_filter = $company_filter = $status_filter = $property_type_filter = "";
$min_price_filter = $max_price_filter = $min_area_filter = $max_area_filter = $down_payment_filter = $payment_period_filter = "";
$unit_design_filter = "";
$view_mode = isset($_GET['view']) && $_GET['view'] === 'card' ? 'card' : 'table';

// Initialize sorting variables
$sort_column = isset($_GET['sort']) ? trim($_GET['sort']) : 'DateAdded';
$sort_direction = isset($_GET['direction']) && $_GET['direction'] === 'desc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';

// Validate sort column to prevent SQL injection
$allowed_sort_columns = [
    'PropertyID' => 'p.PropertyID',
    'DateAdded' => 'p.DateAdded',
    'Price' => 'COALESCE(p.Price, p.min_price)',
    'Area' => 'COALESCE(p.Area, p.avg_area)',
    'ProjectName' => 'p.ProjectName',
    'Title' => 'p.Title',
    'governorate' => 'p.governorate',
    'Status' => 'p.Status'
];

if (!array_key_exists($sort_column, $allowed_sort_columns)) {
    $sort_column = 'DateAdded';
}

// Process search and filter inputs
if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "GET") {
    $search_term = isset($_GET['search']) ? trim($_GET['search']) : '';
    $governorate_filter = isset($_GET['governorate']) ? trim($_GET['governorate']) : '';
    $company_filter = isset($_GET['company']) ? trim($_GET['company']) : '';
    $unit_design_filter = isset($_GET['unit_design']) ? trim($_GET['unit_design']) : '';
    $status_filter = isset($_GET['status']) ? trim($_GET['status']) : '';
    $property_type_filter = isset($_GET['property_type']) ? trim($_GET['property_type']) : '';

    // Advanced filters
    $min_price_filter = isset($_GET['min_price']) && is_numeric($_GET['min_price']) ? trim($_GET['min_price']) : '';
    $max_price_filter = isset($_GET['max_price']) && is_numeric($_GET['max_price']) ? trim($_GET['max_price']) : '';
    $min_area_filter = isset($_GET['min_area']) && is_numeric($_GET['min_area']) ? trim($_GET['min_area']) : '';
    $max_area_filter = isset($_GET['max_area']) && is_numeric($_GET['max_area']) ? trim($_GET['max_area']) : '';
    $down_payment_filter = isset($_GET['down_payment']) ? trim($_GET['down_payment']) : '';
    $payment_period_filter = isset($_GET['payment_period']) ? trim($_GET['payment_period']) : '';
}

// Fetch distinct governorates for the filter dropdown
$governorates_list = [];
$sql_governorates = "SELECT DISTINCT governorate FROM Properties WHERE governorate IS NOT NULL AND governorate != '' ORDER BY governorate";
if ($stmt_gov = $conn->prepare($sql_governorates)) {
    if ($stmt_gov->execute()) {
        $result_governorates = $stmt_gov->get_result();
        while ($row_gov = $result_governorates->fetch_assoc()) {
            $governorates_list[] = $row_gov['governorate'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_gov->close();
}

// Fetch distinct companies for the filter dropdown
$companies_list = [];
$sql_companies = "SELECT DISTINCT company_name FROM Properties WHERE company_name IS NOT NULL AND company_name != '' ORDER BY company_name";
if ($stmt_comp = $conn->prepare($sql_companies)) {
    if ($stmt_comp->execute()) {
        $result_companies = $stmt_comp->get_result();
        while ($row_comp = $result_companies->fetch_assoc()) {
            $companies_list[] = $row_comp['company_name'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_comp->close();
}

// Fetch unit designs for the filter dropdown
$unit_designs = [];
$sql_unit_designs = "SELECT DISTINCT pud.DesignID, pud.DesignName 
                     FROM PropertyUnitDesigns pud 
                     JOIN Properties p ON p.unit_design_id = pud.DesignID 
                     ORDER BY pud.DesignName";
if ($stmt_designs = $conn->prepare($sql_unit_designs)) {
    if ($stmt_designs->execute()) {
        $result_designs = $stmt_designs->get_result();
        while ($row_design = $result_designs->fetch_assoc()) {
            $unit_designs[$row_design['DesignID']] = $row_design['DesignName'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_designs->close();
}

// Fetch property types for the filter dropdown
$property_types = [];
$sql_property_types = "SELECT PropertyTypeID, TypeName FROM PropertyTypes ORDER BY TypeName";
if ($stmt_types = $conn->prepare($sql_property_types)) {
    if ($stmt_types->execute()) {
        $result_types = $stmt_types->get_result();
        while ($row_type = $result_types->fetch_assoc()) {
            $property_types[$row_type['PropertyTypeID']] = $row_type['TypeName'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_types->close();
}

// Build the main SQL query with proper joins for unit design and finishing type
$sql_select = "SELECT p.*, pt.TypeName AS PropertyTypeName, e.FullName AS EmployeeName,
               pud.DesignName AS UnitDesignName, pft.FinishingName AS FinishingTypeName
               FROM Properties p
               LEFT JOIN PropertyTypes pt ON p.PropertyTypeID = pt.PropertyTypeID
               LEFT JOIN Employees e ON p.EmployeeID = e.EmployeeID
               LEFT JOIN PropertyUnitDesigns pud ON p.unit_design_id = pud.DesignID
               LEFT JOIN PropertyFinishingTypes pft ON p.finishing_type_id = pft.FinishingID";
$sql_where = [];
$params = [];
$types = "";

// Add search term filter
if (!empty($search_term)) {
    $sql_where[] = "(p.Title LIKE ? OR p.ProjectName LIKE ? OR p.governorate LIKE ? OR p.district LIKE ? OR p.Location LIKE ? OR p.company_name LIKE ? OR pud.DesignName LIKE ?)";
    $search_param = "%" . $search_term . "%";
    array_push($params, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param);
    $types .= "sssssss";
}

// Add property type filter
if (!empty($property_type_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.PropertyTypeID = ?";
    $params[] = $property_type_filter;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Add governorate filter
if (!empty($governorate_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.governorate = ?";
    $params[] = $governorate_filter;
    $types .= "s";
}

// Add company filter
if (!empty($company_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.company_name = ?";
    $params[] = $company_filter;
    $types .= "s";
}

// Add unit design filter
if (!empty($unit_design_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.unit_design_id = ?";
    $params[] = $unit_design_filter;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Add status filter
if (!empty($status_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.Status = ?";
    $params[] = $status_filter;
    $types .= "s";
}

// Add advanced filters
if (!empty($min_price_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Price, p.min_price) >= ?";
    $params[] = $min_price_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($max_price_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Price, p.max_price) <= ?";
    $params[] = $max_price_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($min_area_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Area, p.min_area) >= ?";
    $params[] = $min_area_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($max_area_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Area, p.max_area) <= ?";
    $params[] = $max_area_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($down_payment_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.down_payment_percentage = ?";
    $params[] = $down_payment_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($payment_period_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.payment_years = ?";
    $params[] = $payment_period_filter;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Add property type filter for commercial properties (multiple types)
$placeholders = str_repeat('?,', count(CURRENT_PROPERTY_TYPE_IDS) - 1) . '?';
$sql_where[] = "p.PropertyTypeID IN ($placeholders)";
foreach (CURRENT_PROPERTY_TYPE_IDS as $type_id) {
    $params[] = $type_id;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Combine SQL parts
$sql = $sql_select;
if (!empty($sql_where)) {
    $sql .= " WHERE " . implode(" AND ", $sql_where);
}

// Add sorting
$sql .= " ORDER BY " . $allowed_sort_columns[$sort_column] . " " . strtoupper($sort_direction);

// Prepare and execute the query
$properties = [];
if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
    if (!empty($params)) {
        $stmt->bind_param($types, ...$params);
    }

    if ($stmt->execute()) {
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
            $properties[] = $row;
        }
    } else {
        error_log("Error executing property query: " . $stmt->error . " | SQL: " . $sql . " | Params: " . print_r($params, true));
        $_SESSION['error_message'] = t('database_error');
    }
    $stmt->close();
} else {
    error_log("Error preparing property query: " . $conn->error . " | SQL: " . $sql);
    $_SESSION['error_message'] = t('db_error_prepare');
}

// Function to format price for display
function formatDisplayPrice($row)
{
    if (!empty($row['Price']) && is_numeric($row['Price'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['Price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_price']) && is_numeric($row['min_price']) && !empty($row['max_price']) && is_numeric($row['max_price'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['min_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' - ' . number_format(floatval($row['max_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_price']) && is_numeric($row['min_price'])) {
        return t('starts_from') . ' ' . number_format(floatval($row['min_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    } elseif (!empty($row['max_price']) && is_numeric($row['max_price'])) {
        return t('up_to') . ' ' . number_format(floatval($row['max_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    }
    return t('price_not_specified');
}

// Function to format area for display
function formatDisplayArea($row)
{
    if (!empty($row['Area']) && is_numeric($row['Area'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['Area']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('sqm');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_area']) && is_numeric($row['min_area']) && !empty($row['max_area']) && is_numeric($row['max_area'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['min_area']), 0, '.', ',') . ' - ' . number_format(floatval($row['max_area']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('sqm');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_area']) && is_numeric($row['min_area'])) {
        return t("starts_from") . " " . number_format(floatval($row["min_area"]), 0, ".", ",") . " " . t("sqm");
    } elseif (!empty($row['max_area']) && is_numeric($row['max_area'])) {
        return t("up_to") . " " . number_format(floatval($row["max_area"]), 0, ".", ",") . " " . t("sqm");
    }
    return t('area_not_specified');
}

// Function to format payment terms
function formatPaymentTerms($row)
{
    $terms = [];
    
    if (!empty($row['down_payment_percentage']) && is_numeric($row['down_payment_percentage'])) {
        $terms[] = number_format($row['down_payment_percentage'], 0) . '% ' . t('down_payment_percentage');
    }
    
    if (!empty($row['payment_years']) && is_numeric($row['payment_years'])) {
        $terms[] = $row['payment_years'] . ' ' . t('years');
    }
    
    return !empty($terms) ? implode(' + ', $terms) : t('undefined');
}

// Function to get status badge HTML
function getStatusBadge($status) {
    $status_classes = [
        'available' => 'status-available',
        'sold' => 'status-sold', 
        'rented' => 'status-rented',
        'unavailable' => 'status-unavailable'
    ];
    
    $status_icons = [
        'available' => 'fas fa-check-circle',
        'sold' => 'fas fa-handshake',
        'rented' => 'fas fa-key',
        'unavailable' => 'fas fa-times-circle'
    ];
    
    $class = $status_classes[$status] ?? 'status-unavailable';
    $icon = $status_icons[$status] ?? 'fas fa-question-circle';
    $text = t('status_' . $status);
    
    return "<span class=\"status-badge {$class}\"><i class=\"{$icon}\"></i> {$text}</span>";
}

// Function to get property type badge HTML
function getPropertyTypeBadge($typeId, $typeName) {
    $type_icons = [
        1 => 'fas fa-home',      // سكني
        2 => 'fas fa-umbrella-beach', // ساحلي
        3 => 'fas fa-building',  // إداري
        4 => 'fas fa-store',     // تجاري
        5 => 'fas fa-hotel',     // فندقي
        6 => 'fas fa-hospital'   // طبي
    ];
    
    $icon = $type_icons[$typeId] ?? 'fas fa-landmark';
    
    return "<span class=\"property-type-badge\"><i class=\"{$icon}\"></i> {$typeName}</span>";
}

<script>
    function toggleAdvancedFilters() {
        const advancedFilters = document.getElementById('advancedFilters');
        const toggleButton = document.querySelector('.advanced-filters-toggle');
        
        if (advancedFilters.classList.contains('show')) {
            advancedFilters.classList.remove('show');
            toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sliders-h"></i> <?php echo t('advanced_filters'); ?>';
        } else {
            advancedFilters.classList.add('show');
            toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-times"></i> <?php echo t('hide_advanced_filters'); ?>';
        }
    }
    
    // Show advanced filters if any of them are set
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        const hasAdvancedFilters = <?php echo
            !empty($min_price_filter) ||
            !empty($max_price_filter) ||
            !empty($min_area_filter) ||
            !empty($max_area_filter) ||
            !empty($down_payment_filter) ||
            !empty($payment_period_filter) ? 'true' : 'false';
        ?>;
        
        if (hasAdvancedFilters) {
            toggleAdvancedFilters();
        }
    });
</script>

<script src="property_row_click.js"></script>

// Session timeout in seconds (e.g., 30 minutes)

FILE: dashboard.php

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . 
'/lang/translate.php'; // Include translation handler
require_once 'session_check.php'; // Include session timeout check
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system

require_once 'db.php'; // Include database connection

// Fetch statistics for the dashboard
$stats = [
    'total_properties' => 0,
    'total_employees' => 0,
    'residential_count' => 0,
    'coastal_count' => 0,
    'commercial_admin_medical_hotel_count' => 0,
];

// Get total properties and counts by type
$sql_properties = "SELECT 
                    COUNT(PropertyID) AS total_count,
                    SUM(CASE WHEN PropertyTypeID = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS residential_count,
                    SUM(CASE WHEN PropertyTypeID = 2 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS coastal_count,
                    SUM(CASE WHEN PropertyTypeID IN (3, 4, 5, 6) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS commercial_admin_medical_hotel_count
                  FROM properties";
if ($result = $conn->query($sql_properties)) {
    $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
    $stats['total_properties'] = $row['total_count'] ?? 0;
    $stats['residential_count'] = $row['residential_count'] ?? 0;
    $stats['coastal_count'] = $row['coastal_count'] ?? 0;
    $stats['commercial_admin_medical_hotel_count'] = $row['commercial_admin_medical_hotel_count'] ?? 0;
    $result->free();
} else {
    error_log("Error fetching property counts: " . $conn->error);
}

// Get total employees (if manager)
if (can_manage_employees()) {
    $sql_employees = "SELECT COUNT(EmployeeID) AS count FROM employees";
    if ($result = $conn->query($sql_employees)) {
        $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
        $stats['total_employees'] = $row['count'] ?? 0;
        $result->free();
    } else {
        error_log("Error fetching employee count: " . $conn->error);
    }
}

?>

        <link rel="stylesheet" href="rtl_styles.css"> 
    <?php endif; ?>
    <style>

FILE: db.php
============================================================
<?php
// Database connection settings
// IMPORTANT: For a production environment, store credentials securely outside the webroot
// (e.g., using environment variables or a dedicated, restricted configuration file).

// Attempt to get credentials from environment variables (best practice)
$host = getenv('DB_HOST') ?: "127.0.0.1";
$user = getenv('DB_USER') ?: "root";
$pass = getenv('DB_PASS') ?: "";
$dbname = getenv('DB_NAME') ?: "broman_db";

// Use mysqli_report to turn errors into exceptions for better handling
mysqli_report(MYSQLI_REPORT_ERROR | MYSQLI_REPORT_STRICT);

try {
    $conn = new mysqli($host, $user, $pass, $dbname);

    // Set character set to UTF-8
    if (!$conn->set_charset("utf8mb4")) {
        // Log the error, but don't necessarily die immediately unless critical
        error_log("Critical Error: Could not set character set utf8mb4: " . $conn->error);
        // Depending on application needs, you might throw an exception or handle this differently
        // For now, we log it and continue, but this is risky for data integrity.
    }

} catch (mysqli_sql_exception $e) {
    // Log the detailed error for administrators
    error_log("Database connection failed: " . $e->getMessage() . " (Code: " . $e->getCode() . ")");

    // Display a generic, user-friendly error message
    // In a real application, you might redirect to an error page or display a message within the layout.
    die("Sorry, we are experiencing technical difficulties. Please try again later or contact support.");
}

// Optional: Remove error reporting back to default if needed elsewhere,
// but generally, letting exceptions bubble up is cleaner.
// mysqli_report(MYSQLI_REPORT_OFF);

FILE: delete_employee.php

<?php
require_once 'session_check.php'; // Include session timeout check
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system

// Check permissions - only manager can manage employees
if (!isset($_SESSION["role"]) || ($_SESSION["role"] !== "admin" && $_SESSION["role"] !== "manager")) {
    $_SESSION["error_message"] = "You do not have permission to access this page.";
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

require_once 'db.php'; // Database connection

$employee_id_to_delete = null;

if (isset($_GET["id"]) && !empty(trim($_GET["id"]))) {
    $employee_id_to_delete = trim($_GET["id"]);

    // Critical: Prevent an admin from deleting their own account, especially if they are the only admin.
    if ($_SESSION["employee_id"] == $employee_id_to_delete) {
        $_SESSION["error_message"] = "You cannot delete your own account.";
        header("Location: employees_list.php");
        exit();
    }

    // Optional: Add a check to prevent deletion of the last admin account if desired.
    // This is a more complex check, ensuring at least one admin remains.
    // For now, we rely on the self-deletion check above.

    // The FK constraint `FK_Properties_Employees` is `ON DELETE SET NULL`,
    // so properties associated with this employee will have their EmployeeID set to NULL.

    $sql = "DELETE FROM Employees WHERE EmployeeID = ?";

    if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
        $stmt->bind_param("i", $param_employee_id);
        $param_employee_id = $employee_id_to_delete;

        if ($stmt->execute()) {
            if ($stmt->affected_rows > 0) {
                $_SESSION["success_message"] = "Employee deleted successfully.";
            } else {
                $_SESSION["error_message"] = "Employee not found or already deleted.";
            }
        } else {
            $_SESSION["error_message"] = "Error deleting employee. Please try again.";
            error_log("Error executing employee delete: " . $stmt->error);
        }
        $stmt->close();
    } else {
        $_SESSION["error_message"] = "Database error (employee delete). Please try again.";
        error_log("Error preparing employee delete: " . $conn->error);
    }
} else {
    $_SESSION["error_message"] = "No employee ID specified for deletion.";
}

// Redirect back to the employee list page
header("Location: employees_list.php");
exit();

FILE: delete_media.php
============================================================
<?php
session_start();
require_once 'db.php';

// Check if user is logged in
if (!isset($_SESSION["loggedin"]) || $_SESSION["loggedin"] !== true) {
    http_response_code(401);
    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Unauthorized']);
    exit;
}

// Check if user is admin (only admins can delete media)
if (!isset($_SESSION["role"]) || $_SESSION["role"] !== "admin") {
    http_response_code(403);
    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Permission denied']);
    exit;
}

// CSRF protection
if (!isset($_POST['csrf_token']) || $_POST['csrf_token'] !== $_SESSION['csrf_token']) {
    http_response_code(403);
    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'CSRF token mismatch']);
    exit;
}

$media_type = $_POST['media_type'] ?? ''; // 'image' or 'audio'
$media_id = $_POST['media_id'] ?? '';

// Validate input
if (!in_array($media_type, ['image', 'audio']) || empty($media_id)) {
    http_response_code(400);
    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Invalid media type or ID']);
    exit;
}

$media_id = intval($media_id);

if ($media_type === 'image') {
    deleteImage($media_id, $conn);
} elseif ($media_type === 'audio') {
    deleteAudio($media_id, $conn);
}

function deleteImage($image_id, $conn) {
    // First, get the file path
    $sql = "SELECT file_path, is_primary FROM property_images WHERE image_id = ?";
    if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
        $stmt->bind_param("i", $image_id);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        
        if ($result->num_rows === 1) {
            $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
            $file_path = $row['file_path'];
            $is_primary = $row['is_primary'];
            $full_path = __DIR__ . '/' . $file_path;
            
            // Delete from database first
            $delete_sql = "DELETE FROM property_images WHERE image_id = ?";
            if ($delete_stmt = $conn->prepare($delete_sql)) {
                $delete_stmt->bind_param("i", $image_id);
                if ($delete_stmt->execute()) {
                    // Delete file from filesystem
                    if (file_exists($full_path)) {
                        unlink($full_path);
                    }
                    
                    // If this was the primary image, set another image as primary
                    if ($is_primary) {
                        $property_sql = "SELECT property_id FROM property_images WHERE image_id = ?";
                        // We need to get property_id before deletion, so let's modify the approach
                        
                        // Get property_id from the original query
                        $stmt->execute(); // Re-execute to get property_id
                        $result = $stmt->get_result();
                        if ($result->num_rows === 1) {
                            $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
                            // Actually, we already deleted the record, so we need a different approach
                        }
                        
                        // Alternative: Get property_id from another image of the same property
                        $update_primary_sql = "UPDATE property_images SET is_primary = 1 WHERE property_id = (SELECT property_id FROM (SELECT property_id FROM property_images WHERE image_id != ? LIMIT 1) as temp) LIMIT 1";
                        // This is complex, let's simplify by getting property_id first
                    }
                    
                    echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Image deleted successfully']);
                } else {
                    http_response_code(500);
                    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Database error: ' . $delete_stmt->error]);
                }
                $delete_stmt->close();
            } else {
                http_response_code(500);
                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Database prepare error: ' . $conn->error]);
            }
        } else {
            http_response_code(404);
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Image not found']);
        }
        $stmt->close();
    } else {
        http_response_code(500);
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Database prepare error: ' . $conn->error]);
    }
}

function deleteAudio($note_id, $conn) {
    // First, get the file path
    $sql = "SELECT file_path FROM property_audio_notes WHERE note_id = ?";
    if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
        $stmt->bind_param("i", $note_id);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        
        if ($result->num_rows === 1) {
            $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
            $file_path = $row['file_path'];
            $full_path = __DIR__ . '/' . $file_path;
            
            // Delete from database
            $delete_sql = "DELETE FROM property_audio_notes WHERE note_id = ?";
            if ($delete_stmt = $conn->prepare($delete_sql)) {
                $delete_stmt->bind_param("i", $note_id);
                if ($delete_stmt->execute()) {
                    // Delete file from filesystem
                    if (file_exists($full_path)) {
                        unlink($full_path);
                    }
                    echo json_encode(['success' => true, 'message' => 'Audio note deleted successfully']);
                } else {
                    http_response_code(500);
                    echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Database error: ' . $delete_stmt->error]);
                }
                $delete_stmt->close();
            } else {
                http_response_code(500);
                echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Database prepare error: ' . $conn->error]);
            }
        } else {
            http_response_code(404);
            echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Audio note not found']);
        }
        $stmt->close();
    } else {
        http_response_code(500);
        echo json_encode(['success' => false, 'message' => 'Database prepare error: ' . $conn->error]);
    }
}

<?php
session_start();
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system

// Check permissions - only manager and admin can delete properties
check_permission('manage_properties');

require_once 'db.php'; // Database connection

$property_id_to_delete = null;

if (isset($_GET["id"]) && !empty(trim($_GET["id"]))) {
    $property_id_to_delete = trim($_GET["id"]);

    // In a production system, a POST request with a CSRF token is safer for delete actions.
    // For this project, we are keeping the GET request for simplicity but adding admin check.

    // The `Properties` table does not have ON DELETE CASCADE for related records like images or features if they were in other tables.
    // Ensure business logic for deleting related data is handled if necessary.

    $sql = "DELETE FROM Properties WHERE PropertyID = ?";

    if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
        $stmt->bind_param("i", $param_property_id);
        $param_property_id = $property_id_to_delete;

        if ($stmt->execute()) {
            if ($stmt->affected_rows > 0) {
                $_SESSION["success_message"] = "Property deleted successfully.";
            } else {
                $_SESSION["error_message"] = "Property not found or already deleted.";
            }
        } else {
            $_SESSION["error_message"] = "Error deleting property. Please try again.";
            error_log("Error executing property delete: " . $stmt->error);
        }
        $stmt->close();
    } else {
        $_SESSION["error_message"] = "Database error (property delete). Please try again.";
        error_log("Error preparing property delete: " . $conn->error);
    }
} else {
    $_SESSION["error_message"] = "No property ID specified for deletion.";
}

// Redirect back to the property list page
header("Location: index_properties.php");
exit();
