FILE: edit_employee.php
============================================================
<?php
require_once 'session_check.php'; // Include session timeout check
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system

// Check permissions - only manager can manage employees
check_permission('manage_employees');

require_once 'db.php'; // Database connection
require_once 'navbar.php'; // Navigation bar

// Initialize variables
$employee_id = $full_name = $username = $role = "";
$full_name_err = $username_err = $password_err = $role_err = $general_err = "";
$is_editing_self = false;

// Check if ID is provided in URL
if (isset($_GET["id"]) && !empty(trim($_GET["id"]))) {
    $employee_id = trim($_GET["id"]);
    if ($_SESSION["employee_id"] == $employee_id) {
        $is_editing_self = true;
    }

    // Prepare a select statement to fetch employee details
    $sql_fetch = "SELECT FullName, Username, Role FROM Employees WHERE EmployeeID = ?";
    if ($stmt_fetch = $conn->prepare($sql_fetch)) {
        $stmt_fetch->bind_param("i", $param_employee_id);
        $param_employee_id = $employee_id;

        if ($stmt_fetch->execute()) {
            $stmt_fetch->store_result();
            if ($stmt_fetch->num_rows == 1) {
                $stmt_fetch->bind_result($full_name, $username, $role);
                $stmt_fetch->fetch();
            } else {
                $_SESSION["error_message"] = "Employee not found.";
                header("Location: employees_list.php");
                exit();
            }
        } else {
            $general_err = "Error fetching employee details.";
            error_log("Error executing employee fetch: " . $stmt_fetch->error);
        }
        $stmt_fetch->close();
    } else {
        $general_err = "Database error (employee fetch). Please try again.";
        error_log("Error preparing employee fetch: " . $conn->error);
    }
} else {
    $_SESSION["error_message"] = "No employee ID specified for editing.";
    header("Location: employees_list.php");
    exit();
}

// Processing form data when form is submitted
if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST") {
    $employee_id_post = trim($_POST["employee_id"]);
    if (empty($employee_id_post) || $employee_id_post != $employee_id) {
        $general_err = "Invalid employee ID submitted.";
    }

    // Validate Full Name
    if (empty(trim($_POST["fullName"]))) {
        $full_name_err = "Please enter the employee's full name.";
    } else {
        $full_name_posted = trim($_POST["fullName"]);
    }

    // Validate Username
    if (empty(trim($_POST["username"]))) {
        $username_err = "Please enter a username.";
    } elseif (!preg_match('/^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$/', trim($_POST["username"]))) {
        $username_err = "Username can only contain letters, numbers, and underscores.";
    } else {
        $sql_check_username = "SELECT EmployeeID FROM Employees WHERE Username = ? AND EmployeeID != ?";
        if ($stmt_check = $conn->prepare($sql_check_username)) {
            $stmt_check->bind_param("si", $param_username_check, $param_employee_id_check);
            $param_username_check = trim($_POST["username"]);
            $param_employee_id_check = $employee_id;
            if ($stmt_check->execute()) {
                $stmt_check->store_result();
                if ($stmt_check->num_rows > 0) {
                    $username_err = "This username is already taken.";
                }
                $username_posted = trim($_POST["username"]);
            } else {
                $general_err = "Error checking username. Please try again.";
                error_log("Error executing username check (edit): " . $stmt_check->error);
            }
            $stmt_check->close();
        } else {
            $general_err = "Database error (username check). Please try again.";
            error_log("Error preparing username check (edit): " . $conn->error);
        }
    }

    // Validate Role
    // Prevent admin from demoting themselves if they are the only admin (basic check)
    if ($is_editing_self && $role === 'admin' && $_POST["role"] === 'user') {
        $sql_count_admins = "SELECT COUNT(EmployeeID) as admin_count FROM Employees WHERE Role = 'admin'";
        $admin_count_result = $conn->query($sql_count_admins);
        $admin_count_row = $admin_count_result->fetch_assoc();
        if ($admin_count_row['admin_count'] <= 1) {
            $role_err = "Cannot demote the only administrator.";
        }
    }
    if (empty($role_err)) { // proceed if no error from self-demotion check
        if (empty($_POST["role"])) {
            $role_err = "Please select a role.";
        } elseif (!in_array($_POST["role"], ["admin", "user", "manager"])) {
            $role_err = "Invalid role selected.";
        } else {
            $role_posted = $_POST["role"];
        }
    }

    // Validate Password (only if new password is provided)
    $new_password = $_POST["new_password"];
    $confirm_password = $_POST["confirm_password"];
    $hashed_password_to_update = null; // Will hold the new hash if password is changed

    if (!empty($new_password)) {
        if (strlen($new_password) < 6) {
            $password_err = "New password must have at least 6 characters.";
        } elseif ($new_password != $confirm_password) {
            $password_err = "New passwords do not match.";
        } else {
            $hashed_password_to_update = password_hash($new_password, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        }
    }

    // Check input errors before updating in database
    if (empty($full_name_err) && empty($username_err) && empty($password_err) && empty($role_err) && empty($general_err)) {
        if ($hashed_password_to_update !== null) {
            $sql_update = "UPDATE Employees SET FullName = ?, Username = ?, Role = ?, Password = ? WHERE EmployeeID = ?";
        } else {
            $sql_update = "UPDATE Employees SET FullName = ?, Username = ?, Role = ? WHERE EmployeeID = ?";
        }

        if ($stmt_update = $conn->prepare($sql_update)) {
            if ($hashed_password_to_update !== null) {
                $stmt_update->bind_param("ssssi", $param_full_name, $param_username, $param_role, $param_password, $param_employee_id_update);
                $param_password = $hashed_password_to_update;
            } else {
                $stmt_update->bind_param("sssi", $param_full_name, $param_username, $param_role, $param_employee_id_update);
            }
            
            $param_full_name = $full_name_posted;
            $param_username = $username_posted;
            $param_role = $role_posted;
            $param_employee_id_update = $employee_id;
            
            if ($stmt_update->execute()) {
                $_SESSION["success_message"] = "Employee details updated successfully!";
                // If admin edited their own role and demoted themselves, log them out or redirect to login
                if ($is_editing_self && $role === 'admin' && $role_posted === 'user') {
                    // Destroy session and redirect to login
                    session_destroy();
                    header("Location: login.php?message=role_changed");
                    exit();
                }
                header("Location: employees_list.php");
                exit();
            } else {
                $general_err = "Something went wrong. Please try again later.";
                error_log("Error executing employee update: " . $stmt_update->error);
            }
            $stmt_update->close();
        }
    } else {
        $full_name = isset($full_name_posted) ? $full_name_posted : $full_name;
        $username = isset($username_posted) ? $username_posted : $username;
        $role = isset($role_posted) ? $role_posted : $role;
    }
}

        <?php if (!empty($password_err)): ?><span class="error-message"><?php echo htmlspecialchars($password_err); ?></span><?php endif; ?>
    </div>

                <div class="form-actions">
                    <input type="submit" value="<?php echo t("save_changes"); ?>" class="btn btn-success">

<?php
// Corrected include path for translation handler
require_once __DIR__ . '/lang/translate.php';
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system

if (session_status() == PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Check permissions - only manager and admin can edit properties
check_permission('manage_properties');

require_once 'db.php'; // Database connection
require_once 'media_functions.php'; // Media helper functions

// Generate CSRF token
$csrf_token = generateCSRFToken();

// Define Property Type IDs
const RESIDENTIAL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID = 1;
const COASTAL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID = 2;
const COMMERCIAL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID = 3;
const ADMINISTRATIVE_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID = 4;
const HOTEL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID = 5;
const MEDICAL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID = 6;

// Initialize variables
$property_id = $title = $property_type_id = $price = $location = $description = $status = "";
$unit_design_id = $finishing_type_id = $compound_area = $down_payment_percentage = $payment_years = $delivery_date = "";
$project_name = $min_price = $max_price = $min_area = $max_area = $avg_area = $district = $governorate = $company_name = $floors_count = $bathrooms = "";
$sales_manager_name = $sales_manager_phone = $salesperson_name = $salesperson_phone = "";

$errors = []; // Array to hold errors

// --- Fetch data for dropdowns ---

// Property Types
$property_types_list = [];
$sql_property_types = "SELECT PropertyTypeID, TypeName FROM PropertyTypes ORDER BY PropertyTypeID";
if ($result_property_types = $conn->query($sql_property_types)) {
    while ($row_type = $result_property_types->fetch_assoc()) {
        $property_types_list[] = $row_type;
    }
    $result_property_types->free();
} else {
    $errors['db_fetch'] = t('error_fetching_property_types') . ': ' . $conn->error;
    error_log("Error fetching property types: " . $conn->error);
}

// Unit Designs
$unit_designs_list = [];
$sql_unit_designs = "SELECT DesignID, DesignName FROM PropertyUnitDesigns ORDER BY DesignName";
if ($result_unit_designs = $conn->query($sql_unit_designs)) {
    while ($row_design = $result_unit_designs->fetch_assoc()) {
        $unit_designs_list[] = $row_design;
    }
    $result_unit_designs->free();
} else {
    $errors['db_fetch'] = t('error_fetching_unit_designs') . ': ' . $conn->error;
    error_log("Error fetching unit designs: " . $conn->error);
}

// Finishing Types
$finishing_types_list = [];
$sql_finishing_types = "SELECT FinishingID, FinishingName FROM PropertyFinishingTypes ORDER BY FinishingName";
if ($result_finishing_types = $conn->query($sql_finishing_types)) {
    while ($row_finish = $result_finishing_types->fetch_assoc()) {
        $finishing_types_list[] = $row_finish;
    }
    $result_finishing_types->free();
} else {
    $errors['db_fetch'] = t('error_fetching_finishing_types') . ': ' . $conn->error;
    error_log("Error fetching finishing types: " . $conn->error);
}


// --- Fetch existing property data ---
if (isset($_GET["id"]) && !empty(trim($_GET["id"]))) {
    $property_id = trim($_GET["id"]);

    $sql_fetch = "SELECT * FROM Properties WHERE PropertyID = ?";
    if ($stmt_fetch = $conn->prepare($sql_fetch)) {
        $stmt_fetch->bind_param("i", $property_id);

        if ($stmt_fetch->execute()) {
            $result = $stmt_fetch->get_result();
            if ($result->num_rows == 1) {
                $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
                // Populate variables with existing data
                $property_type_id = $row["PropertyTypeID"];
                $title = $row["Title"];
                $description = $row["Description"];
                $location = $row["Location"];
                $price = $row["Price"];
                $area = $row["Area"];
                $bathrooms = $row["Bathrooms"];
                $status = $row["Status"];
                $unit_design_id = $row["unit_design_id"];
                $finishing_type_id = $row["finishing_type_id"];
                $compound_area = $row["compound_area"];
                $down_payment_percentage = $row["down_payment_percentage"];
                $payment_years = $row["payment_years"];
                $delivery_date = $row["delivery_date"];
                $project_name = $row["ProjectName"];
                $min_price = $row["min_price"];
                $max_price = $row["max_price"];
                $min_area = $row["min_area"];
                $max_area = $row["max_area"];
                $avg_area = $row["avg_area"];
                $district = $row["district"];
                $governorate = $row["governorate"];
                $company_name = $row["company_name"] ?? '';
                $floors_count = $row["FloorsCount"] ?? null;
                $sales_manager_name = $row["sales_manager_name"] ?? '';
                $sales_manager_phone = $row["sales_manager_phone"] ?? '';
                $salesperson_name = $row["salesperson_name"] ?? '';
                $salesperson_phone = $row["salesperson_phone"] ?? '';
            } else {
                $_SESSION["error_message"] = t('property_not_found');
                header("Location: index_properties.php");
                exit();
            }
        } else {
            $errors['db_fetch'] = t('error_fetching_property_details') . ': ' . $stmt_fetch->error;
            error_log("Error executing property fetch: " . $stmt_fetch->error);
        }
        $stmt_fetch->close();
    } else {
        $errors['db_prepare'] = t('error_preparing_statement') . ': ' . $conn->error;
        error_log("Error preparing property fetch: " . $conn->error);
    }
} else {
    $_SESSION["error_message"] = t('no_property_id_edit');
    header("Location: index_properties.php");
    exit();
}

// Get property media
$property_images = getPropertyImages($property_id, $conn);
$property_audio_notes = getPropertyAudioNotes($property_id, $conn);

// --- Process form submission for update ---
if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST") {
    error_log("POST data: " . print_r($_POST, true)); // Log all POST data
    $property_id_post = trim($_POST["property_id"]);
    if (empty($property_id_post) || $property_id_post != $property_id) {
        $errors['general'] = t('invalid_property_id');
    }

    // Sanitize and retrieve data
    $property_type_id_posted = isset($_POST['property_type_id']) ? trim($_POST['property_type_id']) : '';
    $title_posted = isset($_POST['title']) ? trim($_POST['title']) : '';
    $description_posted = isset($_POST['description']) ? trim($_POST['description']) : null;
    $location_posted = isset($_POST['location']) ? trim($_POST['location']) : null;
    $price_posted = isset($_POST["price"]) && trim($_POST["price"]) !== "" ? trim($_POST["price"]) : null;
    $area_posted = isset($_POST["area"]) && trim($_POST["area"]) !== "" ? trim($_POST["area"]) : null;
    $bathrooms_posted = isset($_POST["bathrooms"]) && trim($_POST["bathrooms"]) !== "" ? trim($_POST["bathrooms"]) : null;
    $status_posted = isset($_POST["status"]) ? trim($_POST["status"]) : "available";
    $unit_design_id_posted = isset($_POST['unit_design_id']) && trim($_POST['unit_design_id']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['unit_design_id']) : null;
    $finishing_type_id_posted = isset($_POST['finishing_type_id']) && trim($_POST['finishing_type_id']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['finishing_type_id']) : null;
    $compound_area_posted = isset($_POST['compound_area']) && trim($_POST['compound_area']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['compound_area']) : null;
    $down_payment_percentage_posted = isset($_POST['down_payment_percentage']) && trim($_POST['down_payment_percentage']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['down_payment_percentage']) : null;
    $payment_years_posted = isset($_POST['payment_years']) && trim($_POST['payment_years']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['payment_years']) : null;
    $delivery_date_posted = isset($_POST['delivery_date']) && trim($_POST['delivery_date']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['delivery_date']) : null;
    $project_name_posted = isset($_POST['project_name']) ? trim($_POST['project_name']) : null;
    $min_price_posted = isset($_POST['min_price']) && trim($_POST['min_price']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['min_price']) : null;
    $max_price_posted = isset($_POST['max_price']) && trim($_POST['max_price']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['max_price']) : null;
    $min_area_posted = isset($_POST['min_area']) && trim($_POST['min_area']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['min_area']) : null;
    $max_area_posted = isset($_POST['max_area']) && trim($_POST['max_area']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['max_area']) : null;
    $avg_area_posted = isset($_POST['avg_area']) && trim($_POST['avg_area']) !== '' ? trim($_POST['avg_area']) : null;
    $district_posted = isset($_POST['district']) ? trim($_POST['district']) : null;
    $governorate_posted = isset($_POST['governorate']) ? trim($_POST['governorate']) : null;
    $company_name_posted = isset($_POST['company_name']) ? trim($_POST['company_name']) : null;
    $floors_count_posted = isset($_POST["floors_count"]) && trim($_POST["floors_count"]) !== '' ? trim($_POST["floors_count"]) : null;
    $sales_manager_name_posted = isset($_POST['sales_manager_name']) ? trim($_POST['sales_manager_name']) : null;
    $sales_manager_phone_posted = isset($_POST['sales_manager_phone']) ? trim($_POST['sales_manager_phone']) : null;
    $salesperson_name_posted = isset($_POST['salesperson_name']) ? trim($_POST['salesperson_name']) : null;
    $salesperson_phone_posted = isset($_POST['salesperson_phone']) ? trim($_POST['salesperson_phone']) : null;
   
    // --- Validation ---
    if (empty($property_type_id_posted)) {
        $errors['property_type_id'] = t('select_property_type');
    }
    if (empty($title_posted)) {
        $errors['title'] = t('enter_property_title');
    }
    if ($price_posted !== null && (!is_numeric($price_posted) || $price_posted < 0)) {
        $errors["price"] = t("enter_valid_price");
    }
    if ($area_posted !== null && (!is_numeric($area_posted) || $area_posted < 0)) {
        $errors["area"] = t("enter_valid_area");
    }
    if ($bathrooms_posted !== null && !filter_var($bathrooms_posted, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, ["options" => ["min_range" => 0]])) {
        $errors["bathrooms"] = t("enter_valid_bathrooms");
    }
    if ($compound_area_posted !== null && (!is_numeric($compound_area_posted) || $compound_area_posted < 0)) {
        $errors["compound_area"] = t("invalid_compound_area");
    }
    if ($down_payment_percentage_posted !== null && (!is_numeric($down_payment_percentage_posted) || $down_payment_percentage_posted < 0 || $down_payment_percentage_posted > 100)) {
        $errors['down_payment_percentage'] = t('invalid_down_payment_percentage');
    }
    if ($payment_years_posted !== null && !filter_var($payment_years_posted, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, ['options' => ['min_range' => 0]])) {
        $errors['payment_years'] = t('invalid_payment_years');
    }
    if ($min_price_posted !== null && (!is_numeric($min_price_posted) || $min_price_posted < 0)) {
        $errors['min_price'] = t('enter_valid_min_price');
    }
    if ($max_price_posted !== null && (!is_numeric($max_price_posted) || $max_price_posted < 0)) {
        $errors['max_price'] = t('enter_valid_max_price');
    }
    if ($min_area_posted !== null && (!is_numeric($min_area_posted) || $min_area_posted < 0)) {
        $errors['min_area'] = t('enter_valid_min_area');
    }
    if ($max_area_posted !== null && (!is_numeric($max_area_posted) || $max_area_posted < 0)) {
        $errors['max_area'] = t('enter_valid_max_area');
    }
    if ($avg_area_posted !== null && (!is_numeric($avg_area_posted) || $avg_area_posted < 0)) {
        $errors['avg_area'] = t('enter_valid_avg_area');
    }
    if ($floors_count_posted !== null && !filter_var($floors_count_posted, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT, ['options' => ['min_range' => 0]])) {
        $errors['floors_count'] = t('invalid_floors_count');
    }

    // If no errors, proceed with update
    if (empty($errors)) {
        $sql_update = "UPDATE Properties SET 
            PropertyTypeID = ?, Title = ?, Description = ?, Location = ?, Price = ?, Area = ?, 
            Bathrooms = ?, Status = ?, unit_design_id = ?, finishing_type_id = ?, 
            compound_area = ?, down_payment_percentage = ?, payment_years = ?, delivery_date = ?, 
            ProjectName = ?, min_price = ?, max_price = ?, min_area = ?, max_area = ?, avg_area = ?, 
            district = ?, governorate = ?, company_name = ?, FloorsCount = ?,
            sales_manager_name = ?, sales_manager_phone = ?, salesperson_name = ?, salesperson_phone = ?
            WHERE PropertyID = ?";

        if ($stmt_update = $conn->prepare($sql_update)) {
            $stmt_update->bind_param(
                "isssddisiiddissdddddsssissssi",
                $property_type_id_posted,
                $title_posted,
                $description_posted,
                $location_posted,
                $price_posted,
                $area_posted,
                $bathrooms_posted,
                $status_posted,
                $unit_design_id_posted,
                $finishing_type_id_posted,
                $compound_area_posted,
                $down_payment_percentage_posted,
                $payment_years_posted,
                $delivery_date_posted,
                $project_name_posted,
                $min_price_posted,
                $max_price_posted,
                $min_area_posted,
                $max_area_posted,
                $avg_area_posted,
                $district_posted,
                $governorate_posted,
                $company_name_posted,
                $floors_count_posted,
                $sales_manager_name_posted,
                $sales_manager_phone_posted,
                $salesperson_name_posted,
                $salesperson_phone_posted,
                $property_id
            );

            if ($stmt_update->execute()) {
                $_SESSION["success_message"] = t('property_updated_success');
                header("Location: view_property.php?id=" . $property_id);
                exit();
            } else {
                $errors['db_update'] = t('error_updating_property') . ': ' . $stmt_update->error;
                error_log("Error executing property update: " . $stmt_update->error);
            }
            $stmt_update->close();
        } else {
            $errors['db_prepare'] = t('error_preparing_statement') . ': ' . $conn->error;
            error_log("Error preparing property update: " . $conn->error);
        }
    }
}

$conn->close();

    <script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            const propertyTypeIdSelect = document.getElementById('property_type_id');
            const residentialCoastalFields = document.getElementById('residential_coastal_fields');
            const commercialFields = document.getElementById('commercial_fields');

            const RESIDENTIAL_ID = <?php echo RESIDENTIAL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID; ?>;
            const COASTAL_ID = <?php echo COASTAL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID; ?>;
            const COMMERCIAL_ID = <?php echo COMMERCIAL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID; ?>;
            const ADMINISTRATIVE_ID = <?php echo ADMINISTRATIVE_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID; ?>;
            const HOTEL_ID = <?php echo HOTEL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID; ?>;
            const MEDICAL_ID = <?php echo MEDICAL_PROPERTY_TYPE_ID; ?>;

            function togglePropertyTypeFields() {
                const selectedTypeId = parseInt(propertyTypeIdSelect.value);

                if (selectedTypeId === RESIDENTIAL_ID || selectedTypeId === COASTAL_ID) {
                    residentialCoastalFields.classList.add('active');
                    commercialFields.classList.remove('active');

                    // Enable/disable required attributes for residential/coastal
                    document.getElementById('unit_design_id').setAttribute('required', 'required');
                    document.getElementById('finishing_type_id').setAttribute('required', 'required');
                    document.getElementById('finishing_type_id_commercial').removeAttribute('required');

                } else if (selectedTypeId === COMMERCIAL_ID || selectedTypeId === ADMINISTRATIVE_ID || selectedTypeId === HOTEL_ID || selectedTypeId === MEDICAL_ID) {
                    residentialCoastalFields.classList.remove('active');
                    commercialFields.classList.add('active');

                    // Enable/disable required attributes for commercial
                    document.getElementById('unit_design_id').removeAttribute('required');
                    document.getElementById('finishing_type_id').removeAttribute('required');
                    document.getElementById('finishing_type_id_commercial').setAttribute('required', 'required');

                } else {
                    residentialCoastalFields.classList.remove('active');
                    commercialFields.classList.remove('active');

                    // Remove required attributes if no specific type is selected
                    document.getElementById('unit_design_id').removeAttribute('required');
                    document.getElementById('finishing_type_id').removeAttribute('required');
                    document.getElementById('finishing_type_id_commercial').removeAttribute('required');
                }
            }

            // Initial call to set correct fields based on loaded property type
            togglePropertyTypeFields();

            // Add event listener for changes
            propertyTypeIdSelect.addEventListener('change', togglePropertyTypeFields);
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ .
'/lang/translate.php'; // Include translation handler
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system

if (session_status() == PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Check permissions - only manager can manage employees
check_permission('manage_employees');

require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php'; // Include database connection using absolute path

$search_term = isset($_GET['search']) ? trim($_GET['search']) : '';

// Prepare SQL to fetch employees using ACTUAL schema column names from broman_db.sql
$sql = "SELECT EmployeeID, FullName, Username, Role, DateJoined FROM employees"; // Corrected column names
if (!empty($search_term)) {
    // Update search fields based on actual schema
    $sql .= " WHERE FullName LIKE ? OR Username LIKE ? OR Role LIKE ?"; // Search by FullName, Username, Role
}
$sql .= " ORDER BY EmployeeID ASC";

$employees = [];
if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
    if (!empty($search_term)) {
        $like_search_term = "%" . $search_term . "%";
        // Bind parameters for new search fields
        $stmt->bind_param("sss", $like_search_term, $like_search_term, $like_search_term);
    }
    if ($stmt->execute()) {
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
            $employees[] = $row;
        }
        $stmt->close();
    } else {
        error_log("Error fetching employees: " . $stmt->error);
        // Use translation key for error message
        $_SESSION['error_message'] = t('error_retrieving_employees');
    }
} else {
    error_log("Error preparing statement for employees: " . $conn->error);
    // Use translation key for error message
    $_SESSION['error_message'] = t('database_error');
}

$conn->close(); // Close connection after fetching data

?>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="<?php echo get_lang_code(); ?>" dir="<?php echo get_direction(); ?>">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title><?php echo t('manage_employees_title'); ?></title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="modern_styles.css">
    <?php if (get_direction() === 'rtl'): ?>
        <link rel="stylesheet" href="rtl_styles.css"> 
    <?php endif; ?>
</head>
<body>

<?php require_once __DIR__ . '/navbar.php'; // Include the navigation bar using absolute path?>

    <div class="container">
        <div class="page-header">
            <h1><?php echo t('employee_management'); ?></h1>
            <a href="add_employee.php" class="btn btn-add">+ <?php echo t('add_new_employee'); ?></a>
        </div>

        <form method="GET" action="employees_list.php" class="search-form">
            <input type="text" name="search" class="form-input" placeholder="<?php echo t('search_employees_placeholder'); ?>" value="<?php echo htmlspecialchars($search_term); ?>">
            <input type="submit" value="<?php echo t('search'); ?>" class="btn btn-primary">
        </form>

        <?php if (isset($_SESSION['error_message'])): ?>
            <p class="error-message"><?php echo htmlspecialchars($_SESSION['error_message']);
            unset($_SESSION['error_message']); ?></p>
        <?php endif; ?>
        <?php if (isset($_SESSION['success_message'])): ?>
            <p class="success-message"><?php echo htmlspecialchars($_SESSION['success_message']);
            unset($_SESSION['success_message']); ?></p>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <?php if (!empty($employees)): ?>
            <div class="table-responsive">
                <table class="data-table">
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th><?php echo t('employee_id'); ?></th>
                            <th><?php echo t('full_name'); // Use correct key if different?></th>
                            <th><?php echo t('username'); ?></th>
                            <th><?php echo t('role'); ?></th>
                            <th><?php echo t('date_joined'); // Use correct key if different?></th>
                            <th><?php echo t('actions'); ?></th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        <?php foreach ($employees as $employee): ?>
                            <tr>
                                <!-- Use ACTUAL schema column names -->
                                <td><?php echo htmlspecialchars($employee['EmployeeID']); ?></td>
                                <td><?php echo htmlspecialchars($employee['FullName']); ?></td>
                                <td><?php echo htmlspecialchars($employee['Username']); ?></td>
                                <td><?php echo t(htmlspecialchars($employee['Role'])); // Translate role?></td> 
                                <td><?php echo htmlspecialchars(date('Y-m-d H:i', strtotime($employee['DateJoined']))); ?></td>
                                <td class="actions">
                                    <!-- Corrected ID parameter -->
                                    <a href="edit_employee.php?id=<?php echo $employee['EmployeeID']; ?>" class="btn btn-edit"><?php echo t('edit'); ?></a>
                                    <?php // Check if the logged-in user is the one being listed to prevent self-deletion
                                    // Use EmployeeID from session if available, otherwise use a placeholder like 'user_id'
                                    $loggedInEmployeeId = $_SESSION['employee_id'] ?? ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? null);
                            if ($loggedInEmployeeId != $employee['EmployeeID']): ?>
                                        <a href="delete_employee.php?id=<?php echo $employee['EmployeeID']; ?>" class="btn btn-delete" onclick="return confirm('<?php echo t('confirm_delete_employee'); ?>');"><?php echo t('delete'); ?></a>
                                    <?php endif; ?>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        <?php endforeach; ?>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        <?php else: ?>
            <p class="no-data">
                <?php echo !empty($search_term) ? t('no_employees_matching_search') : t('no_employees_found'); ?>
                 <a href="add_employee.php"><?php echo t('ask_add_new_employee'); ?></a>
            </p>
        <?php endif; ?>
    </div>

<?php require_once __DIR__ . '/footer.php'; // Include the footer using absolute path?>

// Enhanced sorting functionality for property tables
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // Add sorting functionality to all sortable headers
    const sortableHeaders = document.querySelectorAll('.sortable');
    
    sortableHeaders.forEach(header => {
        // Add accessibility attributes
        header.setAttribute('role', 'button');
        header.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
        
        // Add keyboard support
        header.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
            if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
                e.preventDefault();
                header.click();
            }
        });
        
        // Add visual feedback on click
        header.addEventListener('click', function() {
            // Add loading state
            header.classList.add('sorting-loading');
            
            // Remove loading state after navigation (fallback)
            setTimeout(() => {
                header.classList.remove('sorting-loading');
            }, 1000);
        });
        
        // Add sort hints
        const sortIcon = header.querySelector('.sort-icon');
        if (sortIcon) {
            if (sortIcon.classList.contains('fa-sort')) {
                header.setAttribute('data-sort-hint', 'Click to sort ascending');
            } else if (sortIcon.classList.contains('fa-sort-up')) {
                header.setAttribute('data-sort-hint', 'Click to sort descending');
            } else if (sortIcon.classList.contains('fa-sort-down')) {
                header.setAttribute('data-sort-hint', 'Click to sort ascending');
            }
        }
    });
    
    // Add visual indicators for current sort
    const currentSortColumn = getUrlParameter('sort');
    const currentSortDirection = getUrlParameter('direction');
    
    if (currentSortColumn) {
        sortableHeaders.forEach(header => {
            const onclick = header.getAttribute('onclick');
            if (onclick && onclick.includes(`sort=${currentSortColumn}`)) {
                if (currentSortDirection === 'asc') {
                    header.classList.add('sort-asc');
                } else {
                    header.classList.add('sort-desc');
                }
                header.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
            }
        });
    }
    
    // Enhanced table responsiveness for sorting
    const table = document.querySelector('.properties-table');
    if (table) {
        // Add horizontal scroll indicator for mobile
        const tableContainer = table.closest('.table-responsive');
        if (tableContainer) {
            const scrollIndicator = document.createElement('div');
            scrollIndicator.className = 'scroll-indicator';
            scrollIndicator.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-arrows-alt-h"></i> Scroll horizontally to see more columns';
            
            // Show indicator on mobile if table is wider than container
            function checkScrollIndicator() {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 768 && table.scrollWidth > tableContainer.clientWidth) {
                    if (!tableContainer.querySelector('.scroll-indicator')) {
                        tableContainer.appendChild(scrollIndicator);
                    }
                } else {
                    const indicator = tableContainer.querySelector('.scroll-indicator');
                    if (indicator) {
                        indicator.remove();
                    }
                }
            }
            
            checkScrollIndicator();
            window.addEventListener('resize', checkScrollIndicator);
        }
    }
});

// Helper function to get URL parameters
function getUrlParameter(name) {
    name = name.replace(/[\[]/, '\\[').replace(/[\]]/, '\\]');
    const regex = new RegExp('[\\?&]' + name + '=([^&#]*)');
    const results = regex.exec(location.search);
    return results === null ? '' : decodeURIComponent(results[1].replace(/\+/g, ' '));
}

// Enhanced sorting with smooth transitions
function applySortingTransition() {
    const tableBody = document.querySelector('.properties-table tbody');
    if (tableBody) {
        tableBody.style.transition = 'opacity 0.3s ease';
        tableBody.style.opacity = '0.7';
        
        // Restore opacity after a short delay
        setTimeout(() => {
            tableBody.style.opacity = '1';
        }, 300);
    }
}

// Add CSS for scroll indicator
const scrollIndicatorCSS = `
.scroll-indicator {
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, #17a2b8, #138496);
    color: white;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    text-align: center;
    font-size: 12px;
    border-radius: 0 0 8px 8px;
    margin-bottom: 10px;
    animation: fadeInUp 0.5s ease;
}

.scroll-indicator i {
    margin-right: 5px;
}

@keyframes fadeInUp {
    from {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(10px);
    }
    to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
    }
}

@media (min-width: 769px) {
    .scroll-indicator {
        display: none;
    }
}
`;

// Inject the CSS
const style = document.createElement('style');
style.textContent = scrollIndicatorCSS;
document.head.appendChild(style);

FILE: enhanced_table_functionality.js

// Enhanced JavaScript for Property Table Filtering and Sorting

class PropertyTableManager {
    constructor() {
        this.currentSort = { column: null, direction: 'asc' };
        this.filters = {};
        this.selectedRows = new Set();
        this.init();
    }

    init() {
        this.initSorting();
        this.initFiltering();
        this.initBulkSelection();
        this.initAdvancedFilters();
        this.initQuickFilters();
    }

    // ================================================
    // Sorting Functionality
    // ================================================
    
    initSorting() {
        const sortableHeaders = document.querySelectorAll('.sortable-header');
        sortableHeaders.forEach(header => {
            header.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const column = header.dataset.column;
                this.sortTable(column);
            });
        });
    }

    sortTable(column) {
        const table = document.querySelector('.properties-table tbody');
        const rows = Array.from(table.querySelectorAll('tr'));
        
        // Determine sort direction
        if (this.currentSort.column === column) {
            this.currentSort.direction = this.currentSort.direction === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        } else {
            this.currentSort.direction = 'asc';
        }
        this.currentSort.column = column;

        // Update header indicators
        this.updateSortIndicators();

        // Sort rows
        rows.sort((a, b) => {
            const aValue = this.getCellValue(a, column);
            const bValue = this.getCellValue(b, column);
            
            let comparison = 0;
            if (this.isNumeric(aValue) && this.isNumeric(bValue)) {
                comparison = parseFloat(aValue) - parseFloat(bValue);
            } else {
                comparison = aValue.localeCompare(bValue, undefined, { numeric: true });
            }
            
            return this.currentSort.direction === 'asc' ? comparison : -comparison;
        });

        // Re-append sorted rows
        rows.forEach(row => table.appendChild(row));
        
        // Add visual feedback
        this.addSortAnimation();
    }

    getCellValue(row, column) {
        const cell = row.querySelector(`.cell-${column}`);
        if (!cell) return '';
        
        // Handle special cases
        if (column === 'price' || column === 'area') {
            const text = cell.textContent.trim();
            const numbers = text.match(/[\d,]+/g);
            return numbers ? numbers[0].replace(/,/g, '') : '0';
        }
        
        return cell.textContent.trim();
    }

    isNumeric(value) {
        return !isNaN(parseFloat(value)) && isFinite(value);
    }

    updateSortIndicators() {
        // Reset all indicators
        document.querySelectorAll('.sort-indicator').forEach(indicator => {
            indicator.className = 'sort-indicator none';
        });
        
        // Update current column indicator
        const currentHeader = document.querySelector(`[data-column="${this.currentSort.column}"]`);
        if (currentHeader) {
            const indicator = currentHeader.querySelector('.sort-indicator');
            if (indicator) {
                indicator.className = `sort-indicator ${this.currentSort.direction}`;
            }
            currentHeader.classList.add('sorted');
        }
        
        // Remove sorted class from other headers
        document.querySelectorAll('.sortable-header').forEach(header => {
            if (header.dataset.column !== this.currentSort.column) {
                header.classList.remove('sorted');
            }
        });
    }

    addSortAnimation() {
        const table = document.querySelector('.properties-table');
        table.style.opacity = '0.7';
        setTimeout(() => {
            table.style.opacity = '1';
        }, 150);
    }

    // ================================================
    // Advanced Filtering
    // ================================================
    
    initFiltering() {
        const filterForm = document.querySelector('.filter-section form');
        if (filterForm) {
            // Add real-time filtering for text inputs
            const textInputs = filterForm.querySelectorAll('input[type="text"], input[type="number"]');
            textInputs.forEach(input => {
                let timeout;
                input.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    clearTimeout(timeout);
                    timeout = setTimeout(() => {
                        this.applyFilters();
                    }, 500);
                });
            });

            // Add change listeners for select elements
            const selects = filterForm.querySelectorAll('select');
            selects.forEach(select => {
                select.addEventListener('change', () => {
                    this.applyFilters();
                });
            });
        }
    }

    applyFilters() {
        const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.properties-table tbody tr');
        let visibleCount = 0;

        rows.forEach(row => {
            const isVisible = this.rowMatchesFilters(row);
            row.style.display = isVisible ? '' : 'none';
            if (isVisible) visibleCount++;
        });

        this.updateResultsCount(visibleCount);
        this.updateNoResultsMessage(visibleCount === 0);
    }

    rowMatchesFilters(row) {
        const filters = this.getActiveFilters();
        
        for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(filters)) {
            if (!value) continue;
            
            const cell = row.querySelector(`.cell-${key}`);
            if (!cell) continue;
            
            const cellText = cell.textContent.toLowerCase().trim();
            const filterValue = value.toLowerCase().trim();
            
            if (key === 'search') {
                // Search across all cells
                const allText = Array.from(row.querySelectorAll('td'))
                    .map(td => td.textContent.toLowerCase())
                    .join(' ');
                if (!allText.includes(filterValue)) return false;
            } else if (key.includes('min_') || key.includes('max_')) {
                // Handle numeric range filters
                if (!this.checkNumericFilter(cellText, key, filterValue)) return false;
            } else {
                // Exact or partial match
                if (!cellText.includes(filterValue)) return false;
            }
        }
        
        return true;
    }

    getActiveFilters() {
        const filters = {};
        const form = document.querySelector('.filter-section form');
        if (!form) return filters;

        const inputs = form.querySelectorAll('input, select');
        inputs.forEach(input => {
            if (input.value && input.value.trim()) {
                filters[input.name] = input.value.trim();
            }
        });

        return filters;
    }

    checkNumericFilter(cellText, filterKey, filterValue) {
        const cellNumber = parseFloat(cellText.replace(/[^\d.]/g, ''));
        const filterNumber = parseFloat(filterValue);
        
        if (isNaN(cellNumber) || isNaN(filterNumber)) return true;
        
        if (filterKey.startsWith('min_')) {
            return cellNumber >= filterNumber;
        } else if (filterKey.startsWith('max_')) {
            return cellNumber <= filterNumber;
        }
        
        return true;
    }

    updateResultsCount(count) {
        const countElement = document.querySelector('.results-count .count-number');
        if (countElement) {
            countElement.textContent = count;
        }
    }

    updateNoResultsMessage(show) {
        let noResultsDiv = document.querySelector('.no-results-message');
        
        if (show && !noResultsDiv) {
            noResultsDiv = document.createElement('div');
            noResultsDiv.className = 'no-results-message no-properties';
            noResultsDiv.innerHTML = `
                <i class="fas fa-search"></i>
                <h3>No properties match your filters</h3>
                <p>Try adjusting your search criteria or clearing some filters.</p>
            `;
            document.querySelector('.table-responsive').after(noResultsDiv);
        } else if (!show && noResultsDiv) {
            noResultsDiv.remove();
        }
        
        const table = document.querySelector('.table-responsive');
        if (table) {
            table.style.display = show ? 'none' : 'block';
        }
    }

    // ================================================
    // Bulk Selection
    // ================================================
    
    initBulkSelection() {
        const selectAllCheckbox = document.querySelector('.select-all-checkbox');
        if (selectAllCheckbox) {
            selectAllCheckbox.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                this.selectAll(e.target.checked);
            });
        }

        const bulkCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.bulk-checkbox');
        bulkCheckboxes.forEach(checkbox => {
            checkbox.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                this.toggleRowSelection(e.target.value, e.target.checked);
            });
        });
    }

    selectAll(checked) {
        const visibleCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.bulk-checkbox:not([style*="display: none"])');
        visibleCheckboxes.forEach(checkbox => {
            checkbox.checked = checked;
            this.toggleRowSelection(checkbox.value, checked);
        });
    }

    toggleRowSelection(rowId, selected) {
        if (selected) {
            this.selectedRows.add(rowId);
        } else {
            this.selectedRows.delete(rowId);
        }
        
        this.updateBulkActionsBar();
        this.updateSelectAllState();
    }

    updateBulkActionsBar() {
        const bulkBar = document.querySelector('.bulk-actions-bar');
        const selectionInfo = document.querySelector('.bulk-selection-info');
        
        if (this.selectedRows.size > 0) {
            bulkBar?.classList.add('show');
            if (selectionInfo) {
                selectionInfo.textContent = `${this.selectedRows.size} properties selected`;
            }
        } else {
            bulkBar?.classList.remove('show');
        }
    }

    updateSelectAllState() {
        const selectAllCheckbox = document.querySelector('.select-all-checkbox');
        const visibleCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.bulk-checkbox:not([style*="display: none"])');
        const checkedVisible = Array.from(visibleCheckboxes).filter(cb => cb.checked);
        
        if (selectAllCheckbox) {
            selectAllCheckbox.checked = checkedVisible.length === visibleCheckboxes.length && visibleCheckboxes.length > 0;
            selectAllCheckbox.indeterminate = checkedVisible.length > 0 && checkedVisible.length < visibleCheckboxes.length;
        }
    }

    // ================================================
    // Advanced Filters Panel
    // ================================================
    
    initAdvancedFilters() {
        const toggleButton = document.querySelector('.advanced-filters-toggle');
        if (toggleButton) {
            toggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
                this.toggleAdvancedFilters();
            });
        }
    }

    toggleAdvancedFilters() {
        const panel = document.querySelector('.advanced-filters');
        const toggleButton = document.querySelector('.advanced-filters-toggle');
        
        if (panel && toggleButton) {
            const isVisible = panel.classList.contains('show');
            
            if (isVisible) {
                panel.classList.remove('show');
                toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sliders-h"></i> Advanced Filters';
            } else {
                panel.classList.add('show');
                toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-times"></i> Hide Advanced Filters';
            }
        }
    }

    // ================================================
    // Quick Filters
    // ================================================
    
    initQuickFilters() {
        const quickFilters = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip');
        quickFilters.forEach(chip => {
            chip.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                this.applyQuickFilter(chip);
            });
        });
    }

    applyQuickFilter(chip) {
        // Remove active class from all chips
        document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
        
        // Add active class to clicked chip
        chip.classList.add('active');
        
        // Apply the filter based on chip data
        const filterType = chip.dataset.filter;
        const filterValue = chip.dataset.value;
        
        if (filterType && filterValue) {
            const input = document.querySelector(`[name="${filterType}"]`);
            if (input) {
                input.value = filterValue;
                this.applyFilters();
            }
        }
    }

    // ================================================
    // Export Functionality
    // ================================================
    
    exportSelected(format) {
        if (this.selectedRows.size === 0) {
            alert('Please select properties to export');
            return;
        }
        
        const selectedData = this.getSelectedRowsData();
        
        switch (format) {
            case 'csv':
                this.exportToCSV(selectedData);
                break;
            case 'excel':
                this.exportToExcel(selectedData);
                break;
            case 'pdf':
                this.exportToPDF(selectedData);
                break;
        }
    }

    getSelectedRowsData() {
        const data = [];
        const headers = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.properties-table thead th'))
            .map(th => th.textContent.trim());
        
        this.selectedRows.forEach(rowId => {
            const row = document.querySelector(`tr[data-id="${rowId}"]`);
            if (row) {
                const rowData = Array.from(row.querySelectorAll('td'))
                    .map(td => td.textContent.trim());
                data.push(rowData);
            }
        });
        
        return { headers, data };
    }

    exportToCSV(data) {
        const csv = [data.headers, ...data.data]
            .map(row => row.map(cell => `"${cell}"`).join(','))
            .join('\\n');
        
        const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv' });
        const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = 'properties.csv';
        a.click();
        window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }

    // ================================================
    // Utility Methods
    // ================================================
    
    clearAllFilters() {
        const form = document.querySelector('.filter-section form');
        if (form) {
            form.reset();
            this.applyFilters();
        }
        
        // Clear quick filter selections
        document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(chip => {
            chip.classList.remove('active');
        });
    }

    resetTable() {
        this.clearAllFilters();
        this.selectedRows.clear();
        this.updateBulkActionsBar();
        this.currentSort = { column: null, direction: 'asc' };
        this.updateSortIndicators();
    }
}

// Initialize the table manager when DOM is loaded
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    window.propertyTableManager = new PropertyTableManager();
});

// Additional utility functions for advanced features
function toggleAdvancedFilters() {
    if (window.propertyTableManager) {
        window.propertyTableManager.toggleAdvancedFilters();
    }
}

function exportProperties(format) {
    if (window.propertyTableManager) {
        window.propertyTableManager.exportSelected(format);
    }
}

function clearFilters() {
    if (window.propertyTableManager) {
        window.propertyTableManager.clearAllFilters();
    }
}

<?php
session_start();

// Ensure user is logged in
if (!isset($_SESSION["loggedin"]) || $_SESSION["loggedin"] !== true) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Ensure user is admin
if (!isset($_SESSION["role"]) || $_SESSION["role"] !== "admin") {
    $_SESSION["error_message"] = "You do not have permission to access this page.";
    header("Location: dashboard.php");
    exit;
}

require_once 'db.php'; // Database connection
require_once 'navbar.php'; // Navigation bar

// Helper function to parse numeric values from Excel for DB storage
function parseNumericValueForDB($value_from_excel, $is_price_field = false)
{
    if ($value_from_excel === null || $value_from_excel === '') {
        return null;
    }
    // Remove dots (thousand separators) and any other non-numeric characters except a single decimal point for actual decimals
    $cleaned_value = str_replace('.', '', (string)$value_from_excel);
    // If the original had a comma for decimal, switch it to dot for floatval
    // For this specific Excel, it seems like numbers are 2.200.000 or 160. We assume dots are thousand separators.
    
    if (!is_numeric($cleaned_value)) {
        // Attempt to clean further if it's something like "2,5" for 2.5
        $cleaned_value_comma_decimal = str_replace(',', '.', (string)$value_from_excel);
        if (is_numeric($cleaned_value_comma_decimal)) {
            $numeric_val = floatval($cleaned_value_comma_decimal);
        } else {
            return null; // Cannot parse
        }
    } else {
        $numeric_val = floatval($cleaned_value);
    }

    // Return exact value without division for all fields including price fields
    return $numeric_val;
}

$import_message = "";
$import_error = "";

if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST" && isset($_FILES["excel_file"])) {
    $target_dir = "uploads/";
    
    // Create directory with secure permissions if it doesn't exist
    if (!file_exists($target_dir)) {
        mkdir($target_dir, 0755, true);
    }
    
    $target_file = $target_dir . basename($_FILES["excel_file"]["name"]);
    $fileType = strtolower(pathinfo($target_file, PATHINFO_EXTENSION));
    
    // Check if file is an Excel file
    if ($fileType != "xlsx" && $fileType != "xls") {
        $import_error = "Only Excel files (.xlsx or .xls) are allowed.";
    } else {
        if (move_uploaded_file($_FILES["excel_file"]["tmp_name"], $target_file)) {
            // File uploaded successfully, now process it
            require 'vendor/autoload.php';
            
            try {
                $spreadsheet = \PhpOffice\PhpSpreadsheet\IOFactory::load($target_file);
                $worksheet = $spreadsheet->getActiveSheet();
                $rows = $worksheet->toArray();
                
                // Skip header row
                array_shift($rows);
                
                // Begin transaction
                $conn->begin_transaction();
                
                $properties_added = 0;
                $properties_updated = 0;
                
                foreach ($rows as $row) {
                    // Skip empty rows
                    if (empty($row[0]) && empty($row[1]) && empty($row[2])) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    
                    // Check if property exists by ID or title
                    $property_id = !empty($row[0]) ? intval($row[0]) : null;
                    $title = !empty($row[1]) ? $row[1] : null;
                    
                    $property_exists = false;
                    $existing_id = null;
                    
                    if ($property_id) {
                        $check_sql = "SELECT PropertyID FROM Properties WHERE PropertyID = ?";
                        $check_stmt = $conn->prepare($check_sql);
                        $check_stmt->bind_param("i", $property_id);
                        $check_stmt->execute();
                        $check_result = $check_stmt->get_result();
                        
                        if ($check_result->num_rows > 0) {
                            $property_exists = true;
                            $existing_id = $property_id;
                        }
                        $check_stmt->close();
                    }
                    
                    if (!$property_exists && $title) {
                        $check_sql = "SELECT PropertyID FROM Properties WHERE Title = ?";
                        $check_stmt = $conn->prepare($check_sql);
                        $check_stmt->bind_param("s", $title);
                        $check_stmt->execute();
                        $check_result = $check_stmt->get_result();
                        
                        if ($check_result->num_rows > 0) {
                            $property_exists = true;
                            $row_data = $check_result->fetch_assoc();
                            $existing_id = $row_data['PropertyID'];
                        }
                        $check_stmt->close();
                    }
                    
                    // Parse numeric values
                    $price = parseNumericValueForDB($row[3], true);
                    $area = parseNumericValueForDB($row[4]);
                    $bedrooms = !empty($row[5]) ? intval($row[5]) : null;
                    $bathrooms = !empty($row[6]) ? intval($row[6]) : null;
                    
                    if ($property_exists) {
                        // Update existing property
                        $update_sql = "UPDATE Properties SET 
                                      Title = ?, 
                                      ProjectName = ?, 
                                      Price = ?, 
                                      Area = ?, 
                                      Bedrooms = ?, 
                                      Bathrooms = ?, 
                                      Location = ?, 
                                      Status = ?, 
                                      governorate = ?, 
                                      district = ? 
                                      WHERE PropertyID = ?";
                        
                        $update_stmt = $conn->prepare($update_sql);
                        $update_stmt->bind_param(
                            "ssddiiisssi",
                            $row[1], // Title
                            $row[2], // ProjectName
                            $price,  // Price
                            $area,   // Area
                            $bedrooms, // Bedrooms
                            $bathrooms, // Bathrooms
                            $row[7], // Location
                            $row[8], // Status
                            $row[9], // governorate
                            $row[10], // district
                            $existing_id // PropertyID
                        );
                        
                        if ($update_stmt->execute()) {
                            $properties_updated++;
                        } else {
                            throw new Exception("Error updating property: " . $update_stmt->error);
                        }
                        $update_stmt->close();
                    } else {
                        // Insert new property
                        $insert_sql = "INSERT INTO Properties (Title, ProjectName, Price, Area, Bedrooms, Bathrooms, Location, Status, governorate, district, DateAdded, EmployeeID) 
                                      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NOW(), ?)";
                        
                        $insert_stmt = $conn->prepare($insert_sql);
                        $insert_stmt->bind_param(
                            "ssddiiisssi",
                            $row[1], // Title
                            $row[2], // ProjectName
                            $price,  // Price
                            $area,   // Area
                            $bedrooms, // Bedrooms
                            $bathrooms, // Bathrooms
                            $row[7], // Location
                            $row[8], // Status
                            $row[9], // governorate
                            $row[10], // district
                            $_SESSION["id"] // EmployeeID
                        );
                        
                        if ($insert_stmt->execute()) {
                            $properties_added++;
                        } else {
                            throw new Exception("Error inserting property: " . $insert_stmt->error);
                        }
                        $insert_stmt->close();
                    }
                }
                
                // Commit transaction
                $conn->commit();
                
                $import_message = "Import completed successfully. Added $properties_added new properties and updated $properties_updated existing properties.";
                
                // Delete the uploaded file after processing
                unlink($target_file);
                
            } catch (Exception $e) {
                // Rollback transaction on error
                $conn->rollback();
                $import_error = "Error processing Excel file: " . $e->getMessage();
            }
        } else {
            $import_error = "Sorry, there was an error uploading your file.";
        }
    }
}

<?php
session_start();
require_once __DIR__ . '/lang/translate.php'; // Include translation handler
require_once __DIR__ . '/db.php'; // Database connection
require_once __DIR__ . '/sorting_helpers.php'; // Include sorting helper functions
require_once __DIR__ . '/property_image_helper.php'; // Include image helper functions
require_once 'permissions.php'; // Include permissions system

// Check permissions - all logged in users can view properties
check_permission('view_properties');

// Initialize search and filter variables
$search_term = $governorate_filter = $company_filter = $status_filter = $property_type_filter = "";
$min_price_filter = $max_price_filter = $min_area_filter = $max_area_filter = $down_payment_filter = $payment_period_filter = "";
$unit_design_filter = "";
$view_mode = isset($_GET['view']) && $_GET['view'] === 'card' ? 'card' : 'table';

// Initialize sorting variables
$sort_column = isset($_GET['sort']) ? trim($_GET['sort']) : 'DateAdded';
$sort_direction = isset($_GET['direction']) && $_GET['direction'] === 'desc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';

// Validate sort column to prevent SQL injection
$allowed_sort_columns = [
    'PropertyID' => 'p.PropertyID',
    'DateAdded' => 'p.DateAdded',
    'Price' => 'COALESCE(p.Price, p.min_price)',
    'Area' => 'COALESCE(p.Area, p.avg_area)',
    'ProjectName' => 'p.ProjectName',
    'Title' => 'p.Title',
    'governorate' => 'p.governorate',
    'Status' => 'p.Status'
];

if (!array_key_exists($sort_column, $allowed_sort_columns)) {
    $sort_column = 'DateAdded';
}

// Process search and filter inputs
if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "GET") {
    $search_term = isset($_GET['search']) ? trim($_GET['search']) : '';
    $governorate_filter = isset($_GET['governorate']) ? trim($_GET['governorate']) : '';
    $company_filter = isset($_GET['company']) ? trim($_GET['company']) : '';
    $unit_design_filter = isset($_GET['unit_design']) ? trim($_GET['unit_design']) : '';
    $status_filter = isset($_GET['status']) ? trim($_GET['status']) : '';
    $property_type_filter = isset($_GET['property_type']) ? trim($_GET['property_type']) : '';

    // Advanced filters
    $min_price_filter = isset($_GET['min_price']) && is_numeric($_GET['min_price']) ? trim($_GET['min_price']) : '';
    $max_price_filter = isset($_GET['max_price']) && is_numeric($_GET['max_price']) ? trim($_GET['max_price']) : '';
    $min_area_filter = isset($_GET['min_area']) && is_numeric($_GET['min_area']) ? trim($_GET['min_area']) : '';
    $max_area_filter = isset($_GET['max_area']) && is_numeric($_GET['max_area']) ? trim($_GET['max_area']) : '';
    $down_payment_filter = isset($_GET['down_payment']) ? trim($_GET['down_payment']) : '';
    $payment_period_filter = isset($_GET['payment_period']) ? trim($_GET['payment_period']) : '';
}

// Fetch distinct governorates for the filter dropdown
$governorates_list = [];
$sql_governorates = "SELECT DISTINCT governorate FROM Properties WHERE governorate IS NOT NULL AND governorate != '' ORDER BY governorate";
if ($stmt_gov = $conn->prepare($sql_governorates)) {
    if ($stmt_gov->execute()) {
        $result_governorates = $stmt_gov->get_result();
        while ($row_gov = $result_governorates->fetch_assoc()) {
            $governorates_list[] = $row_gov['governorate'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_gov->close();
}

// Fetch distinct companies for the filter dropdown
$companies_list = [];
$sql_companies = "SELECT DISTINCT company_name FROM Properties WHERE company_name IS NOT NULL AND company_name != '' ORDER BY company_name";
if ($stmt_comp = $conn->prepare($sql_companies)) {
    if ($stmt_comp->execute()) {
        $result_companies = $stmt_comp->get_result();
        while ($row_comp = $result_companies->fetch_assoc()) {
            $companies_list[] = $row_comp['company_name'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_comp->close();
}

// Fetch unit designs for the filter dropdown
$unit_designs = [];
$sql_unit_designs = "SELECT DISTINCT pud.DesignID, pud.DesignName 
                     FROM PropertyUnitDesigns pud 
                     JOIN Properties p ON p.unit_design_id = pud.DesignID 
                     ORDER BY pud.DesignName";
if ($stmt_designs = $conn->prepare($sql_unit_designs)) {
    if ($stmt_designs->execute()) {
        $result_designs = $stmt_designs->get_result();
        while ($row_design = $result_designs->fetch_assoc()) {
            $unit_designs[$row_design['DesignID']] = $row_design['DesignName'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_designs->close();
}

// Fetch property types for the filter dropdown
$property_types = [];
$sql_property_types = "SELECT PropertyTypeID, TypeName FROM PropertyTypes ORDER BY TypeName";
if ($stmt_types = $conn->prepare($sql_property_types)) {
    if ($stmt_types->execute()) {
        $result_types = $stmt_types->get_result();
        while ($row_type = $result_types->fetch_assoc()) {
            $property_types[$row_type['PropertyTypeID']] = $row_type['TypeName'];
        }
    }
    $stmt_types->close();
}

// Build the main SQL query with proper joins for unit design and finishing type
$sql_select = "SELECT p.*, pt.TypeName AS PropertyTypeName, e.FullName AS EmployeeName,
               pud.DesignName AS UnitDesignName, pft.FinishingName AS FinishingTypeName
               FROM Properties p
               LEFT JOIN PropertyTypes pt ON p.PropertyTypeID = pt.PropertyTypeID
               LEFT JOIN Employees e ON p.EmployeeID = e.EmployeeID
               LEFT JOIN PropertyUnitDesigns pud ON p.unit_design_id = pud.DesignID
               LEFT JOIN PropertyFinishingTypes pft ON p.finishing_type_id = pft.FinishingID";
$sql_where = [];
$params = [];
$types = "";

// Add search term filter
if (!empty($search_term)) {
    $sql_where[] = "(p.Title LIKE ? OR p.ProjectName LIKE ? OR p.governorate LIKE ? OR p.district LIKE ? OR p.Location LIKE ? OR p.company_name LIKE ? OR pud.DesignName LIKE ?)";
    $search_param = "%" . $search_term . "%";
    array_push($params, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param, $search_param);
    $types .= "sssssss";
}

// Add property type filter
if (!empty($property_type_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.PropertyTypeID = ?";
    $params[] = $property_type_filter;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Add governorate filter
if (!empty($governorate_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.governorate = ?";
    $params[] = $governorate_filter;
    $types .= "s";
}

// Add company filter
if (!empty($company_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.company_name = ?";
    $params[] = $company_filter;
    $types .= "s";
}

// Add unit design filter
if (!empty($unit_design_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.unit_design_id = ?";
    $params[] = $unit_design_filter;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Add status filter
if (!empty($status_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.Status = ?";
    $params[] = $status_filter;
    $types .= "s";
}

// Add advanced filters
if (!empty($min_price_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Price, p.min_price) >= ?";
    $params[] = $min_price_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($max_price_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Price, p.max_price) <= ?";
    $params[] = $max_price_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($min_area_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Area, p.min_area) >= ?";
    $params[] = $min_area_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($max_area_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "COALESCE(p.Area, p.max_area) <= ?";
    $params[] = $max_area_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($down_payment_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.down_payment_percentage = ?";
    $params[] = $down_payment_filter;
    $types .= "d";
}
if (!empty($payment_period_filter)) {
    $sql_where[] = "p.payment_years = ?";
    $params[] = $payment_period_filter;
    $types .= "i";
}

// Combine SQL parts
$sql = $sql_select;
if (!empty($sql_where)) {
    $sql .= " WHERE " . implode(" AND ", $sql_where);
}

// Add sorting
$sql .= " ORDER BY " . $allowed_sort_columns[$sort_column] . " " . strtoupper($sort_direction);

// Prepare and execute the query
$properties = [];
if ($stmt = $conn->prepare($sql)) {
    if (!empty($params)) {
        $stmt->bind_param($types, ...$params);
    }

    if ($stmt->execute()) {
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        while ($row = $result->fetch_assoc()) {
            $properties[] = $row;
        }
    } else {
        error_log("Error executing property query: " . $stmt->error . " | SQL: " . $sql . " | Params: " . print_r($params, true));
        $_SESSION['error_message'] = t('database_error');
    }
    $stmt->close();
} else {
    error_log("Error preparing property query: " . $conn->error . " | SQL: " . $sql);
    $_SESSION['error_message'] = t('db_error_prepare');
}

// Function to format price for display
function formatDisplayPrice($row)
{
    if (!empty($row['Price']) && is_numeric($row['Price'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['Price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_price']) && is_numeric($row['min_price']) && !empty($row['max_price']) && is_numeric($row['max_price'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['min_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' - ' . number_format(floatval($row['max_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_price']) && is_numeric($row['min_price'])) {
        return t('starts_from') . ' ' . number_format(floatval($row['min_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    } elseif (!empty($row['max_price']) && is_numeric($row['max_price'])) {
        return t('up_to') . ' ' . number_format(floatval($row['max_price']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('currency');
    }
    return t('price_not_specified');
}

// Function to format area for display
function formatDisplayArea($row)
{
    if (!empty($row['Area']) && is_numeric($row['Area'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['Area']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('sqm');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_area']) && is_numeric($row['min_area']) && !empty($row['max_area']) && is_numeric($row['max_area'])) {
        return number_format(floatval($row['min_area']), 0, '.', ',') . ' - ' . number_format(floatval($row['max_area']), 0, '.', ',') . ' ' . t('sqm');
    } elseif (!empty($row['min_area']) && is_numeric($row['min_area'])) {
        return t("starts_from") . " " . number_format(floatval($row["min_area"]), 0, ".", ",") . " " . t("sqm");
    } elseif (!empty($row['max_area']) && is_numeric($row['max_area'])) {
        return t("up_to") . " " . number_format(floatval($row["max_area"]), 0, ".", ",") . " " . t("sqm");
    }
    return t('area_not_specified');
}

// Function to format payment terms
function formatPaymentTerms($row)
{
    $terms = [];
    
    if (!empty($row['down_payment_percentage']) && is_numeric($row['down_payment_percentage'])) {
        $terms[] = number_format($row['down_payment_percentage'], 0) . '% ' . t('down_payment_percentage');
    }
    
    if (!empty($row['payment_years']) && is_numeric($row['payment_years'])) {
        $terms[] = $row['payment_years'] . ' ' . t('years');
    }
    
    return !empty($terms) ? implode(' + ', $terms) : t('undefined');
}

// Function to get status badge HTML
function getStatusBadge($status) {
    $status_classes = [
        'available' => 'status-available',
        'sold' => 'status-sold', 
        'rented' => 'status-rented',
        'unavailable' => 'status-unavailable'
    ];
    
    $status_icons = [
        'available' => 'fas fa-check-circle',
        'sold' => 'fas fa-handshake',
        'rented' => 'fas fa-key',
        'unavailable' => 'fas fa-times-circle'
    ];
    
    $class = $status_classes[$status] ?? 'status-unavailable';
    $icon = $status_icons[$status] ?? 'fas fa-question-circle';
    $text = t('status_' . $status);
    
    return "<span class=\"status-badge {$class}\"><i class=\"{$icon}\"></i> {$text}</span>";
}

// Function to get property type badge HTML
function getPropertyTypeBadge($typeId, $typeName) {
    $type_icons = [
        1 => 'fas fa-home',      // سكني
        2 => 'fas fa-umbrella-beach', // ساحلي
        3 => 'fas fa-building',  // إداري
        4 => 'fas fa-store',     // تجاري
        5 => 'fas fa-hotel',     // فندقي
        6 => 'fas fa-hospital'   // طبي
    ];
    
    $icon = $type_icons[$typeId] ?? 'fas fa-landmark';
    
    return "<span class=\"property-type-badge\"><i class=\"{$icon}\"></i> {$typeName}</span>";
}

<script>
    function toggleAdvancedFilters() {
        const advancedFilters = document.getElementById('advancedFilters');
        const toggleButton = document.querySelector('.advanced-filters-toggle');
        
        if (advancedFilters.classList.contains('show')) {
            advancedFilters.classList.remove('show');
            toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sliders-h"></i> <?php echo t('advanced_filters'); ?>';
        } else {
            advancedFilters.classList.add('show');
            toggleButton.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-times"></i> <?php echo t('hide_advanced_filters'); ?>';
        }
    }
    
    // Show advanced filters if any of them are set
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        const hasAdvancedFilters = <?php echo
            !empty($min_price_filter) ||
            !empty($max_price_filter) ||
            !empty($min_area_filter) ||
            !empty($max_area_filter) ||
            !empty($down_payment_filter) ||
            !empty($payment_period_filter) ? 'true' : 'false';
        ?>;
        
        if (hasAdvancedFilters) {
            toggleAdvancedFilters();
        }
    });
</script>

<script src="property_row_click.js"></script>

</body>
</html>

// Close the database connection
$conn->close();
